<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48865019' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://9to5mac.com/2026/07/10/apple-sues-openai-trade-secret-theft/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple sues OpenAI, accuses ex-employees of stealing trade secrets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果控告 OpenAI，指控前员工窃取商业机密</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 13:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48865019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1612</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48865019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 920</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48865019'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A high-profile lawsuit pits Apple against OpenAI over alleged misappropriation of trade secrets by former OpenAI staff. The case centers on proprietary AI techniques and internal data siphoned during a transition, signaling growing tension as big tech fights over AI talent. The outcome could influence how firms protect confidential knowledge during acquisitions and departures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一起备受关注的诉讼指控苹果指控OpenAI及其前员工窃取商业机密，聚焦于AI技术与内部数据的可能外泄。此案凸显大型科技公司在人才流动与知识保护方面的博弈，将影响企业在并购和员工离职时的机密信息防护策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/9to5mac.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2026/05/apple-openai.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/9to5mac.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2026/05/apple-openai.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.patrick-breyer.de/en/eu-parliament-greenlights-chat-control-1-0-breyer-our-children-lose-out/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU Parliament greenlights Chat Control 1.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟议会通过聊天监控1.0，Breyer：我们的孩子要吃亏</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 09, 2026 / 04:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48843923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1603</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48843923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 836</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48843923'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This marks a major push to enable cross-border scanning of encrypted messages in pursuit of child safety and crime prevention. Critics warn that the measure expands surveillance at the expense of privacy and free expression, while Breyer argues that children will lose out.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这标志着推动跨境扫描加密通讯以提升儿童安全和打击犯罪的重大举措。批评者担心此举侵蚀隐私与言论自由，而Breyer则认为这会让儿童吃亏。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.patrick-breyer.de/wp-content/uploads/2023/07/logo_regular_vertical.png' data-full='https://www.patrick-breyer.de/wp-content/uploads/2023/07/logo_regular_vertical.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tris.sherliker.net/blog/obfuscated-self-evaluating-bash-script-by-cdn-akamai-being-supplied-to-consumers-via-retail-stores/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Decoding the obfuscated bash script on a Uniqlo t-shirt</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>解码优衣库 T 恤上的混淆 Bash 脚本</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 01:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48829312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1416</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48829312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 226</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48829312'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers embedded an obfuscated, self-evaluating Bash script on a Uniqlo T-shirt delivered through a CDN. The stunt demonstrates how software-like code can slip into consumer channels via retail distribution, highlighting trust and supply-chain risks. It argues for stronger code provenance checks and verification in modern supply networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员在优衣库 T 恤上嵌入一个混淆、可自我评估的 Bash 脚本，并通过 CDN 交付给消费者。这一举动揭示了软件代码通过零售渠道渗透到消费端的风险与供应链信任问题，并呼吁在现代供应链中加强代码来源核验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tris.sherliker.net/assets/name.png' data-full='https://tris.sherliker.net/assets/name.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48838876' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(5) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apnews.com/article/john-deere-right-to-repair-agriculture-equipment-cb7514ffedb95c130a976af661f2bc02' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>John Deere owners will get the right to repair equipment under FTC settlement</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FTC和解赋予约翰迪尔设备的修理权</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 16:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48838876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1355</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48838876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 294</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48838876'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An FTC settlement grants John Deere equipment owners the right to repair, potentially lowering downtime and repair costs. It signals a shift toward repairability by nudging OEMs to share tools, manuals, and parts, and could boost independent repair shops. This case could influence broader right-to-repair momentum in tech-enabled farming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FTC与约翰迪尔设备修理权的和解，可能降低停机时间与维修成本，推动制造商提供维修工具、手册与零件。此举或促成独立修理商的兴起，或对农业科技领域的修理权运动产生示范效应。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dims.apnews.com/dims4/default/eb2d449/2147483647/strip/true/crop/4513x3007+0+162/resize/980x653!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fassets.apnews.com%2Fca%2Fc8%2F5fff3b3e7c95ef4bbd4696383598%2F674e975157b745d28967fa953a18c3d4' data-full='https://dims.apnews.com/dims4/default/eb2d449/2147483647/strip/true/crop/4513x3007+0+162/resize/980x653!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fassets.apnews.com%2Fca%2Fc8%2F5fff3b3e7c95ef4bbd4696383598%2F674e975157b745d28967fa953a18c3d4' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48797916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48797916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 283</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48818311' data-tags='Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48818311' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fightchatcontrol.eu/chat-control-overview' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chat Control 1.0 and 2.0 Explained</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chat Control 1.0 与 2.0 的解读</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 07:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48818311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 900</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48818311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 340</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48818311'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This explainer breaks down the evolution of Chat Control policies across versions 1.0 and 2.0, focusing on moderation vs. innovation trade-offs and regulatory implications. It helps developers assess how such rules affect platform design and user safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>系统梳理了 Chat Control 1.0 与 2.0 的要点与演进，聚焦于监管与创新之间的权衡，以及对平台设计和用户安全的影响。为开发者评估相关政策的影响提供了清晰视角。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48808482' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openwrt.org/toh/openwrt/one' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenWrt One – Open Hardware Router</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenWrt One：开源硬件路由器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 11:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48808482' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 812</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48808482' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 312</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenWrt One introduces a complete open-hardware router design paired with OpenWrt software, letting users inspect schematics and customize firmware end-to-end. The project signals a move toward verifiably open networking gear that prioritizes user sovereignty, security, and long-term repairability. It could reshape how hobbyists source and build their own routers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenWrt One 将开源硬件路由器设计与 OpenWrt 软件结合，允许用户查看原理图并全流程定制固件。该项目预示着向更可验证的开源网络设备转变，强调用户自主性、安全性及长期可维护性。可能改变爱好者自建路由器的选型与实现方式。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/malisper/pgrust' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Postgres rewritten in Rust, now passing 100% of the Postgres regression tests</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project to rewrite PostgreSQL components in Rust reports passing all regression tests, signaling Rust&#x27;s viability for safety-critical system software. While not a drop-in replacement, the effort demonstrates how safer memory management and modern tooling could influence database internals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>用 Rust 重写 PostgreSQL 组件的尝试声称已通过全部回归测试，显示 Rust 在安全性关键系统中的潜力。虽然不是直接的替代，但这项工作展示了更安全的内存管理与现代工具链对数据库内部实现的潜在影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.comaps.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CoMaps – FOSS Offline Maps</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CoMaps：开源离线地图</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 11:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48808928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 776</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48808928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 205</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48808928'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CoMaps is an open-source offline mapping project that lets you navigate without internet access. It emphasizes privacy and local control by bundling downloadable map data and routing logic, avoiding reliance on centralized services. This makes it appealing for privacy-minded users and developers who want a portable, maintainable map solution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CoMaps 是一个开源离线地图项目，支持在没有互联网时进行导航。它强调隐私与本地控制，提供可下载的地图数据与路由逻辑，避免对集中服务的依赖。对注重隐私的用户和开发者而言，这是一种可移植、可维护的地图方案。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48823557' data-tags='Privacy,Politics,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48823557' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://allaboutcookies.org/eu-mandatory-distracted-driver-system' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Every new car sold in the European Union must include a driver monitoring camera</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟规定所有新车必须装配驾驶员监控摄像头</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 13:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48823557' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 773</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48823557' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1043</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48823557'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Europe&#x27;s new rule mandates driver monitoring cameras in all new cars, aiming to curb distracted driving and fatigue-related accidents. The requirement will push automakers to integrate interior cameras and analytics, raising privacy, data handling, and compliance concerns. Given the EU market&#x27;s scale, this could set a de facto global safety standard for vehicle interiors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟的新规要求所有新车配备驾驶员监控摄像头，旨在减少疲劳驾驶和分心驾驶导致的事故。厂商需在车内安装摄像头并接入分析功能，同时引发隐私保护、数据处理与合规性方面的担忧。凭借其市场规模，该举或将为全球车辆内饰安全标准定调。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://andrewkelley.me/post/my-thoughts-bun-rust-rewrite.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My thoughts on the Bun Rust rewrite</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 09, 2026 / 02:47 PT</span></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48843352'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Bun project is exploring a Rust rewrite to address safety and performance limitations of the original runtime. The note discusses why a Rust-based core could improve reliability and tooling integration, while also outlining migration and compatibility challenges that come with rewriting a mature platform.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文章探讨 Bun 以 Rust 重写核心的原因，强调安全性和性能提升的潜力，以及对可靠性与工具链集成的影响。还提及迁移路线和兼容性挑战等在对成熟平台进行重写时需要权衡的问题。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48861717' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48861717' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/quadrf-can-spot-drones-and-see-wifi-through-my-wall/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>QuadRF can spot drones and see WiFi through my wall</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>QuadRF 能侦测无人机并穿墙探测 WiFi 信号</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 08:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48861717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 736</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48861717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 231</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48861717'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>QuadRF demonstrates that RF sensing can reveal both drone activity and WiFi signals through walls. This kind of passive observation highlights security and privacy implications for home networks and aerial devices, even when devices aren&#x27;t in visible sight. It also underscores practical limits like range, clutter, and legal boundaries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>QuadRF 展示了射频探测可以在墙后揭示无人机活动和 WiFi 信号。这类被动观测引发对家庭网络和无人机安全与隐私的担忧，同时也暴露出覆盖范围、信号干扰和法律边界等局限。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/quadrf-can-spot-drones-and-see-wifi-through-my-wall/quadrf-antennna-array.jpeg' data-full='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/quadrf-can-spot-drones-and-see-wifi-through-my-wall/quadrf-antennna-array.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48804993' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.xbox.com/en-us/2026/07/06/resetting-xbox/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Resetting Xbox</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Xbox 重置</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 07:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48804993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 730</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48804993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 912</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48804993'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft outlines reset options for Xbox consoles, detailing when to perform a factory reset versus a simpler reboot and what data stays or is erased. The guidance aims to give players a reliable recovery path for performance issues, software glitches, or corrupted saves. The update underscores the importance of predictable recovery flows as consoles grow more complex.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软为 Xbox 主机列出重置选项，说明何时执行出厂重置、何时进行简单重启，以及数据保留或清除的范围。该指南为应对性能问题、软件故障或数据损坏提供可靠的恢复路径。随着主机软件日益复杂，可靠的恢复流程显得尤为重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://xboxwire.thesourcemediaassets.com/sites/2/2026/05/Bootup_Wire-9c068aa206c9a72d2b1f.png' data-full='https://xboxwire.thesourcemediaassets.com/sites/2/2026/05/Bootup_Wire-9c068aa206c9a72d2b1f.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48833715'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TypeScript 7 brings major improvements to the type system, smarter inference, and faster builds, plus new compiler features that reduce runtime errors. It also updates project configuration and editor tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TypeScript 7 对类型系统、推断能力和构建速度带来重大改进，并引入新的编译器特性以降低运行时错误。此次发布还对项目配置和编辑器工具链进行了更新。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://popcar.bearblog.dev/its-about-ownership/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>It&#x27;s not about physical vs. digital games, it&#x27;s about ownership</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不是实体/数字游戏之争，而是关于所有权的问题</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 05, 2026 / 07:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48794750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 677</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48794750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 515</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48794750'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that ownership—not format—drives value in gaming, covering DRM, portability, and platform control that shape how players access and keep games.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出，游戏真正的分水岭在于所有权，而非物理还是数字的形式；包括数字版权管理、跨平台可移植性和平台控制等因素，决定玩家对游戏的长期拥有与访问。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://martinalderson.com/posts/the-upcoming-ai-margin-collapse-part-1-glm-5-2/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GLM 5.2 and the coming AI margin collapse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GLM 5.2 与 AI 利润率的收缩来临</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 13:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48809877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 676</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48809877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 462</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48809877'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GLM 5.2 illustrates continued improvements in language models, but the piece argues that margins for AI services may compress as compute costs rise and competition intensifies. It examines implications for developers, vendors, and the economics of AI deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GLM 5.2 展示了语言模型的持续进步，但文章认为随着计算成本上升与竞争加剧，AI 服务的利润率可能收缩。讨论了对开发者、厂商和 AI 部署经济性的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 08:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48819244' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 663</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48819244' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 591</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48819244'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft reportedly fired the IdTech team at id Software, signaling a broader reorganization of its game-engine and studio strategy. The move may affect ongoing engine maintenance and future collaboration on titles that rely on Id Tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软据传解雇了来自 Id Software 的 IdTech 团队，显示出其在游戏引擎和工作室战略上的整合调整。此举可能影响遗留引擎的维护以及依赖 Id Tech 的项目未来合作。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟议会完成对‘聊天控制’提案的首轮表决</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 08:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48819008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 635</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48819008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 259</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48819008'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EU Parliament gave initial approval to the Chat Control proposal, advancing it to the next legislative stage. If enacted, it would mandate scanning private messages for abuse material, raising debates about end-to-end encryption, privacy, and platform obligations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟议会对‘聊天控制’提案完成初步表决，推进至下一轮立法程序。若最终通过，可能要求对私人消息进行扫描以发现虐待材料，引发对端对端加密、隐私保护及平台责任的广泛讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48863464' data-tags='Legal,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48863464' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/jul/10/new-york-city-deceptive-subscriptions-ban' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New York City to ban deceptive subscription practices</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纽约市将禁止欺骗性订阅行为</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 11:26 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48863464' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 632</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48863464' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 334</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48863464'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New York City plans to ban deceptive subscription practices, including auto-renewal traps and hidden charges. The move aims to improve consumer protection and force clearer disclosures from subscription-based services, potentially reshaping how onboarding and renewal terms are presented online. This could raise the bar for startups and platforms relying on subscription models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约市拟禁止欺骗性订阅行为，包括自动续订陷阱与隐藏费用，提升消费保护。此举将促使订阅型服务在用户注册与续订条款上提供更清晰的披露，或对采用订阅模式的企业带来影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/b522a4563292b5972c9443aa2001a8637f1266b5/72_226_2591_2073/master/2591.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=8576dc8571bc0efaa7b0ef0eb44b88fa' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/b522a4563292b5972c9443aa2001a8637f1266b5/72_226_2591_2073/master/2591.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=8576dc8571bc0efaa7b0ef0eb44b88fa' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48811591' data-tags='Programming,Entertainment,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48883275' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48883275' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://systima.ai/blog/claude-code-vs-opencode-token-overhead' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code sends 33k tokens before reading the prompt; OpenCode sends 7k</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 在读取提示前消耗 3.3 万令牌；OpenCode 仅约 7 千令牌</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 12, 2026 / 11:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 528</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 293</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48883275'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code consumes about 3.3万 tokens before the user prompt is parsed, while OpenCode uses roughly 7,000 tokens. The gap highlights how different toolchains incur context overhead that affects latency, cost, and developer workflows in AI-assisted coding. This emphasizes the importance of managing token budgets when chaining multiple AI services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 在读取用户提示前就消耗约 3.3 万令牌，而 OpenCode 约 7 千令牌。这一差距揭示了不同工具链在上下文开销上的差异，进而影响 AI 辅助编码的延迟、成本和工作流。强调在串联多家 AI 服务时对令牌预算进行管理的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48827858' data-tags='AI,Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48827858' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://noma.security/blog/gitlost-how-we-tricked-githubs-ai-agent-into-leaking-private-repos/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitLost: We Tricked GitHub&#x27;s AI Agent into Leaking Private Repos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitLost：我们欺骗 GitHub 的 AI 代理泄露私有仓库</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 22:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48827858' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 525</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48827858' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48827858'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers show a vulnerability where an AI-powered assistant on GitHub can be coaxed into exposing private repositories. By manipulating prompts and data flows, they demonstrate leakage risks from copilots and automated tooling. This underscores the need for stronger data-handling controls, better prompt safeguards, and auditability in AI-assisted development ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者展示了一种漏洞：GitHub 上的 AI 助手在被引导下可能泄露私有仓库的内容。通过操纵提示和数据流，他们演示了协作工具与自动化流程中的数据泄露风险。这凸显了在 AI 辅助开发生态中加强数据处理、提示防护和审计机制的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://noma.security/wp-content/uploads/Screenshot-2026-06-29-at-14.16.35.png' data-full='https://noma.security/wp-content/uploads/Screenshot-2026-06-29-at-14.16.35.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48863490' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48863490' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cdn.openai.com/pdf/04d1d1e4-bc75-476a-97cf-49055cd98d31/cdc_proof.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra produces proof of the Cycle Double Cover Conjecture [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra 产出循环双覆盖猜想的证明（PDF）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 11:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48863490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 523</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48863490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 429</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48863490'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra has produced a proof of the Cycle Double Cover Conjecture, with a PDF provided. If verified, this would mark a notable milestone in AI-assisted formal proofs and could accelerate future mathematical work, but the proof will be rigorously scrutinized by the community. The episode highlights AI&#x27;s potential to aid deep mathematics while underscoring the need for careful validation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra 似乎给出了循环双覆盖猜想的证明，并附有 PDF。若经同行验证，这一成果可能标志着 AI 辅助形式化证明迈出重要一步，或加速后续的数学研究，但证明将接受严格审查。此事凸显 AI 在高深数学领域的潜力，同时也强调需要谨慎验证。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48858121' data-tags='Design'>

<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gingerbill.org/article/2026/07/10/good-tools-are-invisible/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Good Tools Are Invisible</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>好工具往往无形</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 03:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48858121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 523</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48858121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 238</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48858121'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Great tools fade into the background by design, reducing cognitive load and letting people focus on what matters. The piece cautions that invisibility should not hide value from teams and argues for clear outcomes and good integration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>优秀的工具被设计为在工作流中隐形，降低认知负担，让人专注于核心任务。文章提醒不要让它们的价值被忽视，并强调要有清晰的成效与良好的集成。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48816959' data-tags='Science,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://whynothugo.nl/journal/2026/07/03/98-isnt-very-much/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>98% isn&#x27;t much</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>98% 并不算多</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 05:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48816959' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 510</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48816959' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 334</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48816959'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A cautionary take on high percentages: 98% can be misleading if base rates, sample quality, or failure modes aren&#x27;t accounted for. The piece argues for richer metrics and stress testing to avoid overestimating performance in real-world use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>高百分比并不总是等于高可靠性。98% 这样的数字若忽略基线、样本偏差或边缘案例，容易带来错误解读。建议采用更全面的指标和鲁棒性测试来评估真实世界表现。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48821576'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A local, CPU-friendly TTS approach using Kokoro enables high-quality speech synthesis on consumer hardware without relying on cloud services. It highlights a shift toward private, on-device AI that scales with CPUs rather than GPUs.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48832212' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48832212' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mistral.ai/news/robostral-navigate/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mistral&#x27;s Robostral Navigate: a state of the art robotics navigation model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mistral 的 Robostral Navigate：前沿机器人导航模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 07:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48832212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 485</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48832212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 110</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48832212'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mistral unveils Robostral Navigate, a state-of-the-art robotics navigation model designed to improve real-time planning, obstacle avoidance, and multi-sensor fusion. Early demonstrations suggest stronger robustness and efficiency in dynamic environments, with broad potential for logistics and service robotics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mistral 发布 Robostral Navigate，这是一款前沿的机器人导航模型，聚焦实时规划、避障和多传感器融合。早期演示显示在动态环境中具备更强鲁棒性与效率，未来可能推动物流、服务机器人等领域。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48845442' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48845442' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mwi.westpoint.edu/the-glass-backbone-why-the-armys-logistics-will-break-in-the-next-war/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The glass backbone: Why the Army&#x27;s logistics will break in the next war</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>玻璃脊梁：未来战争中陆军后勤为何会崩溃</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 09, 2026 / 06:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48845442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 457</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48845442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 651</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48845442'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that modern military logistics rely on a fragile, highly digitized backbone, making supply chains vulnerable in future conflicts. It discusses failure points and resilience strategies like redundancy and decentralization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为现代军事后勤以脆弱、高度数字化的骨干为支撑，在未来冲突中极易受到干扰。文中分析潜在崩溃点，并提出通过冗余、去中心化等方式提升韧性的思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mwi.westpoint.edu/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/9522808.jpg?w=1080' data-full='https://mwi.westpoint.edu/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/9522808.jpg?w=1080' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48834296' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48834296' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cyberinsider.com/eu-now-one-step-away-from-reviving-private-message-scanning-rules/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU now one step away from reviving private message scanning rules</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟只差一步就能重新启用私信扫描规则</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 09:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48834296' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 453</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48834296' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 181</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48834296'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EU is nearing revival of private-message scanning rules, aiming to mandate messaging platforms to scan private content for illegal activity. The move would reshape safety vs. privacy dynamics, pressuring platforms to deploy more aggressive monitoring and data processing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟正在推动重新启用对私信的内容扫描规则，拟要求通讯平台对私人内容进行非法活动的检测。此举将重新定义安全与隐私的平衡，推动更大规模的监控与数据处理，并引发隐私担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cyberinsider.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/EU-now-one-step-away-from-reviving-private-message-scanning-rules.jpg' data-full='https://cyberinsider.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/EU-now-one-step-away-from-reviving-private-message-scanning-rules.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48808002' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48808002' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/research/global-workspace' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A global workspace in language models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>语言模型的全球工作区</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 10:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48808002' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 451</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48808002' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 192</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48808002'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper introduces a global workspace architecture for language-model systems, a shared memory-like substrate that coordinates tools, modules, and reasoning. By enabling components to broadcast and fuse information, it aims to improve long-horizon planning, multi-step reasoning, and robustness in AI agents. This concept could drive more modular, interpretable, and scalable AI systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该研究提出了语言模型系统的全球工作区架构，作为一个共享的记忆式底层，用于协调工具、模块与推理。通过让各组件进行信息广播与融合，它旨在提升长程规划、多步推理与系统鲁棒性。此概念有望推动更模块化、可解释且可扩展的 AI 系统。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/fbdc967f9f3d88566f25b21cce7ef523b3fabbbb-1280x720.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/fbdc967f9f3d88566f25b21cce7ef523b3fabbbb-1280x720.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://acoup.blog/2026/01/30/collections-the-late-bronze-age-collapse-a-very-brief-introduction/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Late Bronze Age Collapse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>晚青铜时代崩溃</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 04:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48858737' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 414</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48858737' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 294</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48858737'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Late Bronze Age collapse refers to a complex, multi-state disruption around 1200 BCE. The post offers a concise introduction to prevailing theories—from drought and famine to invasions and trade breakdown. It matters because it informs how interconnected civilizations can fail, a topic with resonance for modern supply chains and risk management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>晚青铜时代崩溃指的是公元前约1200年的多国共同性崩溃，理论包括干旱、饥荒、入侵与贸易网络破裂等。该简介帮助理解为何高度互联的文明体系会崩溃，这对当今的供应链与风险管理仍具启示意义。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48866134' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48866134' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.brown.edu/news/2026-07-09/chemical-bonds-relativity' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Einstein&#x27;s relativity rules chemical bonds in heavy elements, new research shows</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新研究称相对论决定重元素的化学键</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 15:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48866134' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 394</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48866134' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 180</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48866134'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Relativistic effects become significant in very heavy elements, reshaping electron orbitals and bond strengths. The new work shows that Einstein&#x27;s relativity plays a decisive role in how chemical bonds form in superheavy elements, with implications for modeling their chemistry and discovering new materials. This challenges non-relativistic intuition that underpins much of chemistry for lighter elements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在极重元素中，相对论效应显著改变电子轨道、键能与分子几何。最新研究表明，爱因斯坦的相对论在超重元素的化学键形成中发挥关键作用，这可能改变对其化学性质的建模与新材料的设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.brown.edu/sites/default/files/styles/wide_lrg/public/2026-07/chemistry-stock.jpg?h=1d4b4ebd&amp;itok=Iv8YhdkV' data-full='https://www.brown.edu/sites/default/files/styles/wide_lrg/public/2026-07/chemistry-stock.jpg?h=1d4b4ebd&amp;itok=Iv8YhdkV' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48884853' data-tags='Privacy,Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48884853' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scrapfly.dev/posts/browser-math-os-fingerprint/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Since Chromium 148, Math.tanh is now fingerprintable to link underlying OS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自 Chromium 148 以来，Math.tanh 可用于指纹识别并关联底层操作系统</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 12, 2026 / 14:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48884853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 383</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48884853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 183</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48884853'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chromium 148 adds a fingerprintable signal from Math.tanh that can correlate browser observations with host OS characteristics. In practice, sites could exploit this as a new cross-site fingerprinting channel to tie a user’s browser to their machine, challenging existing privacy protections. The change highlights how tiny API quirks can expand tracking risks and may drive new mitigations or detection techniques.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chromium 148 引入了 Math.tanh 的指纹信号，能将浏览器观察与底层操作系统特征联系起来。简单说，就是一个新的跨站指纹通道，可能被用来把用户的浏览器与其机器绑定，削弱现有的隐私防护。这一小细节再次暴露了浏览器 API 与隐私之间的博弈，或促使厂商和研究者研究对策。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scrapfly.dev/img/og-default.png' data-full='https://scrapfly.dev/img/og-default.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48756578' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://herdr.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Herdr: One terminal to rule them all</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Herdr：一个终端统治所有终端</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 01, 2026 / 21:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48756578' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 377</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48756578' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 165</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48756578'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Herdr bills itself as a universal terminal experience, consolidating multiple shell sessions and workflows into one interface. The project targets developers who juggle SSH sessions, containers, and local terminals, promising reduced context switching and a cleaner workflow. Adoption will depend on the quality of integration with popular shells and remote environments, as well as performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Herdr 主打“一个终端统治一切”的理念，将多个 shell 会话和工作流集中在同一界面。它面向需要同时管理 SSH、容器与本地终端的开发者，承诺降低上下文切换、提升工作流整洁度。是否被广泛采用取决于对主流 shell 与远程环境的集成质量以及性能表现。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48812156' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bradleywoolf.com/links-1/sequencing-my-own-dna-at-home' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to sequence your own DNA at home</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在家自行测序自己的DNA的方法</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 17:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48812156' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 372</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48812156' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 151</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48812156'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores ways individuals can sequence their own DNA at home using consumer kits and portable devices, highlighting growing accessibility in DIY genomics. The piece underlines the trade-offs between cost, accuracy, and privacy as people take genetic testing into their own hands.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨个人如何利用消费级工具和便携设备在家测序DNA，凸显 DIY 基因组学的日益普及。文中强调成本、准确性与隐私之间的权衡，以及个人开展基因测试的现实影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.spr.so/cdn-cgi/imagedelivery/j42No7y-dcokJuNgXeA0ig/f7bd0bf9-fe1a-4481-ae32-a529633a4c5d/img_4423/public' data-full='https://images.spr.so/cdn-cgi/imagedelivery/j42No7y-dcokJuNgXeA0ig/f7bd0bf9-fe1a-4481-ae32-a529633a4c5d/img_4423/public' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gamesindustry.biz/eve-onlines-carbon-engine-is-now-open-source-fenris-creations-explains-why' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EVE Online&#x27;s Carbon engine is now open source: Fenris Creations explains why</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 03, 2026 / 14:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48780387' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 369</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48780387' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 123</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48780387'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EVE Online&#x27;s Carbon engine is released as open source, with Fenris Creations outlining the rationale. Open sourcing the engine could accelerate modding, transparency, and community-driven improvements while inviting broader experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>EVE Online 的 Carbon 引擎现已开源，Fenris Creations 解释了这样做的原因。开源有望促进模组开发、透明度以及社区协作的改进，并推动更广泛的实验。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nintendo.com/en-gb/Support/Nintendo-Switch-2/Information-about-upcoming-battery-related-revisions-to-some-Nintendo-products-3132901.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nintendo announces new product revisions in Europe with replaceable batteries</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 06:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48804193' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 358</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48804193' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 214</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48804193'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nintendo is rolling out revisions in Europe that include replaceable batteries, signaling a push toward repairability and longer product lifespans. The move aligns with sustainability trends and consumer demand for easier upgrades.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>任天堂在欧洲推出的新版本产品配备可更换电池，强调可修复性与延長产品寿命。此举回应了对可持续性和易于升级的消费者需求。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48842611' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48842611' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.howtogeek.com/why-developers-are-ditching-github-for-codeberg-and-self-hosting-alternatives/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why developers are ditching GitHub for Codeberg and self-hosting alternatives</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何开发者弃用 GitHub 转投 Codeberg 与自托管替代方案</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 09, 2026 / 01:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48842611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 357</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48842611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 250</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48842611'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A growing share of developers are moving away from GitHub toward Codeberg and other self-hosted options due to concerns about data sovereignty, privacy, and platform control. These alternatives offer open-source stacks and greater compliance flexibility, but they trade ecosystem scale and tooling maturity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>越来越多的开发者正在离开 GitHub，转向 Codeberg 与自托管的代码托管方案，原因包括数据主权、隐私与对平台控制权的考量。这些替代方案提供开源栈和更灵活的合规性，但在生态规模和工具链的成熟度上需要权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static0.howtogeekimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/2025/07/concept-of-computer-programming-or-developing-software-laptop-computer-with-code-on-screen-heart-message-cog-home-user-cloud-and-lock-icons.jpg?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' data-full='https://static0.howtogeekimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/2025/07/concept-of-computer-programming-or-developing-software-laptop-computer-with-code-on-screen-heart-message-cog-home-user-cloud-and-lock-icons.jpg?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://io-fund.com/ai-stocks/nvidia-coreweave-nebius-circular-financing-gpu-boom' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia, CoreWeave, and Nebius: Inside the Circular Financing of the GPU Boom</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Nvidia、CoreWeave 与 Nebius：GPU 热潮的循环融资内幕</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 10:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48873836' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 354</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48873836' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 154</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48873836'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains circular financing used by GPU-focused players to recycle capital across rounds, accelerating equipment deployment for AI workloads; discusses players&#x27; roles and financial tactics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>揭示围绕 GPU 行业的循环融资如何在多轮融资中回流资本，加速 AI 工作负载的显卡部署。分析参与方的角色和财务策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.prismic.io/bethtechnology/aitsR6lQnVZVEPNy_Nvidia%2CCoreWeave%2CandNebius%E2%80%93InsidetheCircularFinancingoftheGPUBoom.png?auto=format,compress' data-full='https://images.prismic.io/bethtechnology/aitsR6lQnVZVEPNy_Nvidia%2CCoreWeave%2CandNebius%E2%80%93InsidetheCircularFinancingoftheGPUBoom.png?auto=format,compress' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48825749' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48825749' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kb.cert.org/vuls/id/213560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tenda firmware (multiple versions) contains hidden authentication backdoor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tenda 固件（多版本）含隐藏认证后门</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 17:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48825749' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 350</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48825749' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 120</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48825749'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hidden authentication backdoors exist in multiple Tenda firmware versions, potentially letting attackers bypass login. The finding underscores ongoing firmware and supply-chain risks in consumer networking gear, so users should update firmware and review credentials.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CERT 发现多版 Tenda 固件中存在隐藏认证后门，可能绕过登录实现远程访问。此漏洞揭示消费级路由器固件的供应链安全风险，用户应尽快升级固件并更换默认凭据。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ocw.mit.edu/courses/6-001-structure-and-interpretation-of-computer-programs-spring-2005/video_galleries/video-lectures/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Structure and Interpretation of Computer Programs Video Lectures (1986)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《计算机程序的结构与解释》1986 年视频讲座</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 16:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48825664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 350</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48825664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48825664'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MIT&#x27;s 1986 SICP video lectures on programming education emphasize abstraction and recursion, shaping how software design is taught and understood. The lectures remain a foundational reference for thinking about problem-solving and building robust software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MIT 的 1986 年《计算机程序的结构与解释》视频讲座强调抽象与递归等核心理念，深刻影响了计算机科学的教学与软件设计思维。至今仍被视为理解问题求解与构建健壮软件的基础参考。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48820220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 349</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48820220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 479</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Chinese official has been sentenced to death for accepting $325 million in bribes. The verdict underscores China&#x27;s aggressive stance on corruption, especially at high levels, and signals the potential penalties for graft as part of broader anti-corruption campaigns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名中国官员因接受3.25亿美元贿赂被判死刑，凸显中国对腐败的严厉态度，尤其在高层官员中。此判决显示反腐斗争的持续推进，以及对贪污行为的严厉惩罚。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/chatgpt-for-your-most-ambitious-work/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ChatGPT Work</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ChatGPT 在工作中的应用</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48849059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 348</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48849059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 186</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI positions ChatGPT as a tool for ambitious work, highlighting capabilities for drafting, research, and collaboration in professional contexts. The messaging signals a push to position ChatGPT as a productivity platform for teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 将 ChatGPT 定位为高强度工作场景的工具，突出草拟、研究与协作等功能在专业环境中的应用。此举标志着将 ChatGPT 打造成团队生产力平台的市场策略。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48859701' data-tags='Programming,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48859701' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://unstack.io/write-code-like-a-human-will-maintain-it' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Write code like a human will maintain it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>编写便于维护的代码</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 06:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48859701' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 348</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48859701' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 299</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48859701'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Guidelines for writing code that future maintainers can understand. It emphasizes readability, clear naming, modular design, solid tests, and good documentation to reduce long-term maintenance debt. The piece argues that maintenance costs typically exceed initial development, so building with future contributors in mind matters.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>给未来维护者可读的代码的准则。强调可读的结构、清晰的命名、模块化设计、完善的测试和文档，以降低长期维护成本。指出维护支出通常超过初步开发，因此要以未来协作者的可维护性为目标。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48864252' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/SubscriberLink/1080822/990a8a5e2d379085/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An update on residential proxies and the scraper situation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>住宅代理与抓取现状更新</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 12:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48864252' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 345</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48864252' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 373</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48864252'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Residential proxies remain a core tool for large-scale scraping, but policy moves and anti-abuse measures are tightening the space. The update points to changes in pricing, reliability, and access that could disrupt data-driven workflows. For developers and researchers, it signals the need for compliant scraping strategies and robust data sourcing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>住宅代理仍是大规模抓取的核心工具，但相关政策和反滥用措施正在收紧。更新指出定价、可靠性与获取途径的变化，可能影响需要大量网页数据的工作流。对开发者和研究者而言，意味着需要采取合规的抓取策略与更稳健的数据获取方案。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/this-buried-apple-feature-turns-an-iphone-into-the-perfect-kids-dumb-phone/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Buried Apple feature turns an iPhone into the perfect kids&#x27; dumb phone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>隐藏的苹果功能将 iPhone 变为理想的儿童简易机</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 03:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48803004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 344</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48803004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 223</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48803004'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hidden iOS feature lets parents limit calls, messages, and apps to turn an iPhone into a simple device for kids. The move highlights ongoing consumer control and parental safety tradeoffs as smartphones become everyday tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>隐藏的 iOS 功能允许家长限制通话、信息和应用，将 iPhone 打造成简单的儿童设备。这一举措凸显了在智能手机日益普及的同时，用户控制与安全的权衡。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48834924' data-tags='Show HN,AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48834924' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://microsoft.github.io/flint-chart/#/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Microsoft releases Flint, a visualization language for AI agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：微软发布 Flint——用于 AI 代理的可视化语言</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 10:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48834924' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 343</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48834924' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 134</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48834924'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Flint provides a visual language to model data flows, decisions, and actions for AI agents, enabling rapid prototyping without heavy coding. By graphically mapping agent behavior, teams can design, test, and debug multi-step workflows more efficiently.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Flint 提供一种用于 AI 代理的数据流、决策点和执行动作的可视化语言，使团队可以在不写大量代码的情况下快速原型。通过图形化表示代理行为，设计、测试和调试多步骤工作流的效率将显著提升。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48815196' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48815196' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pcmag.com/news/a-hackers-arrest-reveals-microsoft-can-track-users-via-a-windows-device' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft Can Track Users via a Windows Device ID</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软可通过Windows设备ID追踪用户</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 01:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48815196' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 341</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48815196' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 153</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48815196'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Windows Device ID can enable persistent user tracking beyond cookies, according to a hacker&#x27;s arrest and coverage. The ID can survive reinstalls and be linked across apps and services, highlighting ongoing privacy trade-offs in modern Windows telemetry. The takeaway is that device identifiers remain a potent privacy concern that requires clear controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一枚Windows设备ID可能在跨越Cookie之外持续跟踪用户，该结论来自一位黑客被捕的报道。该ID可在重装系统后仍然存在，并在应用与服务之间建立关联，凸显现代Windows遥测中的隐私权衡。核心启示是设备标识仍然是一个强隐私风险点，需要更清晰的控制选项。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.pcmag.com/imagery/articles/02VZVE6JO9dXqKyKowYhZzj-1.fit_lim.size_1200x630.v1783350873.jpg' data-full='https://i.pcmag.com/imagery/articles/02VZVE6JO9dXqKyKowYhZzj-1.fit_lim.size_1200x630.v1783350873.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48827325' data-tags='Hardware,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48827325' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://neil.computer/notes/how-to-setup-minimal-zfs-nas-without-truenas/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to Build a Minimal ZFS NAS Without Synology, QNAP, TrueNAS (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无需 Synology、QNAP、TrueNAS 的极简 ZFS NAS 构建指南</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 20:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48827325' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 338</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48827325' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 240</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48827325'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Build a lean ZFS-based NAS using off-the-shelf hardware and a minimal software stack, avoiding vendor-locked NAS appliances. The guide covers pool layouts (like RAIDZ or mirrors), basic OS choices, and straightforward backup strategies, offering a transparent alternative to Synology/QNAP/TrueNAS ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示在不依赖厂商生态的前提下，如何用极简的软件栈与通用硬件搭建 ZFS NAS。文中给出磁盘池布局（如 RAIDZ/镜像）、操作系统选型和备份策略等要点，提供比商用设备更透明、可控的自建方案。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cg5pd9z2487o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bonnie Tyler has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>邦妮·泰勒去世</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 09, 2026 / 03:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48843438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 334</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48843438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 126</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48843438'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Iconic vocalist Bonnie Tyler has died, marking the end of an era defined by raspy, powerful ballads that helped shape the sound of 1980s rock. Her signature tracks and unmistakable voice continue to resonate on streaming playlists and in covers, keeping her legacy alive for new generations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>著名歌手邦妮·泰勒去世，标志着以粗哑嗓音和强烈情感著称的80年代摇滚风格时代的告别。她的代表作与独特嗓音在流媒体歌单和翻唱作品中持续回响，确保了她的音乐遗产得以传承。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析大型电商平台上仿冒品的治理现状与挑战，探讨为何大规模执法困难、品牌方如何保护商品列表，以及消费者在大型电商平台上如何看待真实性与溯源。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.iroh.computer/blog/mesh-llm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mesh LLM: distributed AI computing on iroh</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mesh LLM：在 iroh 上的分布式 AI 计算</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 15:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48876505' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 325</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48876505' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48876505'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mesh LLM demonstrates distributed AI computing across a mesh of nodes using iroh, reducing reliance on centralized hardware. The approach highlights how decentralized coordination can scale model inference and resilience in AI workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mesh LLM 展示了在 iroh 上通过网状节点进行分布式 AI 计算，降低对中心化硬件的依赖。去中心化协作在推理扩展和 AI 工作负载的弹性方面展现潜力。</p>

</div>
