<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48811644' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ternlight-demo.vercel.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ternlight – 7 MB embedding model that runs in browser (WASM)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ternlight——7 MB 嵌入式模型在浏览器(WASM)中运行</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 16:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48811644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 322</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48811644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48811644'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Presents a compact embedding model of just 7 MB that runs entirely in the browser via WebAssembly, enabling private, client-side NLP tasks. The project signals progress toward ultra-lightweight, privacy-preserving AI for web apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍一个仅 7 MB 的嵌入式模型，借助 WebAssembly 完整在浏览器端运行，支持私有化的本地 NLP 任务。该项目标志着前端超轻量化 AI 与隐私保护能力的持续提升。</p>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48778956' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 312</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48778956' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 223</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48778956'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta is reusing older RAM in new servers by pairing it with a custom bridge chip to cut costs and improve sustainability. The approach raises questions about reliability, performance consistency, and upgrade paths in hyperscale deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 在新服务器中以定制桥芯片搭配，重复利用旧RAM，降低成本并提升数据中心的可持续性。此举也引发关于可靠性、性能一致性及未来升级路径的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://datacenter.iers.org/data/latestVersion/bulletinC.txt' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>No leap second will be introduced at the end of December 2026</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2026 年底将不再加入闰秒</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 09, 2026 / 07:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48846281' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 308</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48846281' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 243</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48846281'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The International Earth Rotation and Reference Systems Service confirms no leap second will be added at the end of 2026. This continues a trend toward simpler, predictable timekeeping for distributed systems. Engineers should plan around a leap-second-free timeline while staying alert for policy updates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>国际时刻参考系统宣布 2026 年底不再添加闰秒。这延续了简化且可预测的全球时间计量趋势，便于分布式系统的时钟同步。工程师应在无闰秒的时间线下开展计划，并关注未来的政策变动。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48863064' data-tags='Space,Business,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48863064' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.zdnet.com/home-and-office/networking/spacex-wants-to-launch-100000-more-starlink-satellites/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SpaceX wants to launch 100k more Starlink satellites for 100x the bandwidth</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SpaceX计划再发射十万颗 Starlink 卫星以实现百倍带宽</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 10:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48863064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 306</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48863064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48863064'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SpaceX aims to deploy up to 100,000 new Starlink satellites to boost total bandwidth by around 100x. The plan would dramatically reshape global connectivity, but faces colossal challenges in propulsion, spectrum, orbital debris, and regulatory approvals. If realized, it could push internet access to remote regions while stressing space logistics and climate concerns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SpaceX 计划再发射十万颗 Starlink 卫星，带宽有望实现约100倍的提升。此举可能彻底改变全球连接格局，但在推进、频谱、轨道碎片与监管方面仍面临巨大挑战。若实现，将使偏远地区上网更易，但也对太空运营与环境提出新压力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.zdnet.com/a/img/resize/2d7049a36d241cdf177935c0c0df5f03a2d52bf7/2026/07/10/f550fe8f-37ce-4eb1-b39c-c1e306d05a74/gettyimages-2280481321.jpg?auto=webp&amp;fit=crop&amp;height=675&amp;width=1200' data-full='https://www.zdnet.com/a/img/resize/2d7049a36d241cdf177935c0c0df5f03a2d52bf7/2026/07/10/f550fe8f-37ce-4eb1-b39c-c1e306d05a74/gettyimages-2280481321.jpg?auto=webp&amp;fit=crop&amp;height=675&amp;width=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48873692' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/jul/04/california-hawaii-rowing-solo-journey' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Female US rower completes historic solo journey from California to Hawaii</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美籍女性单人划艇完成加州到夏威夷的历史性旅程</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 10:03 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://connectsci.au/news/news-parent/9703/Spider-venom-kills-varroa-mites-without-harming' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Spider venom kills varroa mites without harming honeybees</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蜘蛛毒素能杀死瓦螨而不伤蜜蜂</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 22:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48841259' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 300</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48841259' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 140</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48841259'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Spider venom has been shown to kill Varroa destructor mites without harming honeybees, offering a potential breakthrough for beekeeping. If scalable, it could reduce colony losses and chemical miticide use, though delivery methods and safety will matter for adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>某种蜘蛛毒素可杀死瓦螨而不伤蜜蜂，为养蜂业带来或许的突破。若能规模化，可能降低蜂群损失与化学杀螨剂的使用，但投放方式、特异性与安全性将决定其推广程度。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://odra.dev/slopfix/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We charge $10k a week to delete AI-generated code</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 13:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48823359' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 300</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48823359' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 233</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48823359'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A service charges $10k per week to scrub AI-generated code from repositories, addressing concerns about licensing, attribution, and ownership of machine-generated code. It highlights how organizations are already negotiating how to handle AI-derived assets and maintain auditable codebases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一家服务机构收取每周1万美元为代码库清除 AI 生成代码，聚焦于许可、署名与所有权等问题。此举反映企业在如何管理 AI 派生资产、并保持可审计代码库方面所面临的挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.euronews.com/culture/2026/06/29/dua-lipa-opens-library-for-banned-and-censored-books-in-portugal' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dua Lipa opens library for banned and censored books in Portugal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>杜阿·利帕在葡萄牙开设被禁书籍图书馆</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 05:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48817017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 298</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48817017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48817017'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dua Lipa opens a library in Portugal focused on banned and censored titles, spotlighting censorship issues and giving readers access to restricted titles. The move blends advocacy and culture, encouraging conversations about free expression and the power of libraries as spaces for challenge and learning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>杜阿·利帕在葡萄牙开设了专门收录被禁及审查书籍的图书馆，聚焦言论自由问题并为读者提供受限书籍的获取途径。此举将倡导与文化结合起来，推动关于自由表达的讨论，并凸显图书馆在促进挑战性与学习方面的作用。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48854168' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/06/30/the-father-of-the-internet-is-finally-retiring/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vint Cerf, “father of the Internet”, is retiring</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>互联网之父 Vint Cerf 将退休</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 09, 2026 / 17:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48854168' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 294</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48854168' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 169</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48854168'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vint Cerf, a founding figure of the Internet and a long-time advocate for open standards, is stepping away from public roles. His retirement signals a generational shift in Internet governance as new platforms and threats emerge. Cerf’s career underscores the enduring link between protocol design, policy, and global connectivity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>互联网之父、长期倡导开放标准的 Vint Cerf 将从公开岗位退下。此举标志着互联网治理的世代转变，面对新平台与新威胁，行业将迎来新的领导力。 Cerf 的职业生涯强调了协议设计、政策制定与全球互联的持久联系。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/05/vint-cerf-GettyImages-454796419.jpg?resize=1200,723' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/05/vint-cerf-GettyImages-454796419.jpg?resize=1200,723' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/07/apple-to-increase-spend-with-broadcom-to-produce-billions-more-us-chips/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple to increase spend with Broadcom to produce billions more U.S. chips</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果将增加对博通的支出，在美国生产更多数十亿颗芯片</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 04:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48830565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 289</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48830565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 232</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48830565'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple plans to increase spending with Broadcom to produce billions more chips in the United States, reinforcing the domestic manufacturing push enabled by policy incentives. The expansion deepens Apple-Broadcom collaboration, signaling stronger supply resilience and potential price dynamics for Apple&#x27;s chips.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果将增加对博通的支出，在美国生产更多数十亿颗芯片，强化政府激励下的本土制造战略。此举巩固了苹果与博通的长期合作，提升供应韧性并可能影响芯片定价与供给格局。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48834919' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.flatpanelshd.com/news.php?subaction=showfull&amp;id=1783340582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PlayStation can delete all your digital games after 3 years of inactivity (EU)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟规则可能使 PlayStation 在3年不活跃后删除所有数字游戏</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 10:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48834919' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 284</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48834919' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 153</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48834919'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>European rules could allow platforms like PlayStation to delete all digital games after three years of inactivity, prompting concerns about digital ownership and long-term access. The move underscores how regulatory compliance can reshape access to purchased content and the importance of retention and export options.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟规定可能允许平台在账户三年不活跃后删除数字游戏，PlayStation 可能因此删除全部数字藏品，引发对数字所有权和长期访问的担忧。这一举动凸显监管要求如何改变对已购内容的访问，并凸显留存与导出选项的重要性。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48856904' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48856904' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nevo-project.epfl.ch/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI-generated videos to maximally drive a target brain region</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 生成视频以最大化激活目标脑区</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 00:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48856904' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 279</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48856904' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 233</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48856904'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers are using AI-generated videos to selectively stimulate a target brain region, aiming to drive neural activity more efficiently with visual input. The approach could advance noninvasive brain stimulation and brain–computer interfaces, but it also raises questions about safety, biases in generative models, and ethical implications of neural manipulation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员利用 AI 生成的视频来有选择地激活某一脑区，试图通过视觉刺激更高效地驱动神经活动。该技术若成熟可推动非侵入性脑刺激和脑机接口的发展，同时也引发关于安全、生成模型偏差与神经操控伦理等问题的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48845514' data-tags='Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48845514' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/07/us-seeks-cheaper-hunter-killer-drones-after-iran-destroys-1b-worth-of-reapers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US seeks cheaper hunter-killer drones after Iran destroys $1B worth of Reapers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国在伊朗摧毁价值10亿美元的 Reaper 无人机后寻求更便宜的猎杀型无人机</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 09, 2026 / 06:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48845514' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 275</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48845514' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 333</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48845514'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>After Iran destroyed a $1 billion fleet of MQ-9 Reaper drones, the US is pursuing cheaper hunter-killer alternatives to bolster resilience and reduce single-point vulnerability. The move highlights how high-value platforms can be cost-prohibitive to replace and may accelerate shifts toward modular, lower-cost, and potentially more autonomous systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在伊朗摧毁价值10亿美元的 MQ-9 Reaper 无人机后，美国正寻求成本更低的猎杀型替代方案以提升韧性并降低单点脆弱性。此举凸显高价值平台的替换成本高，可能加速向模块化、低成本以及更具自主性的系统转型。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Reaper-drone-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Reaper-drone-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48824826' data-tags='Science,Politics,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48824826' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gao.gov/products/gao-26-108193' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GAO: DOE Is Prematurely Excluding Less Expensive Options for Nuclear Cleanup</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DOE 在核清理中过早排除更便宜的选项</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 15:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48824826' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 274</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48824826' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 172</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48824826'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GAO finds DOE is prematurely narrowing nuclear cleanup options, risking higher lifecycle costs and delays. The report urges a broader, risk-informed evaluation of alternatives and earlier consideration of cheaper, proven methods. This matters for taxpayers and cleanup timelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GAO 指出能源部在核废物清理选项的评估上过早做出排除，可能推高全生命周期成本、延误项目。报告建议采用更广泛、以风险为基础的备选方案评估，并尽早考量更便宜、经验证的方法。这对纳税人和清理时间线至关重要。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48815982' data-tags='Politics,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48815982' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(107) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dw.com/en/germany-migrants-skilled-workers-integration-labor-market-bureaucracy-language-housing/a-77853162' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why skilled workers come to Germany and then leave again</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何熟练工人来到德国却又离开</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 03:42 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48815982' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 274</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48815982' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 763</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48815982'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis of why skilled workers migrate to Germany but often depart, citing bureaucracy, housing, language barriers, and integration hurdles that affect retention.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析熟练劳动者为何涌向德国却常常离开，涉及繁文缥缈的行政、住房、语言与融入等方面的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48831658' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48831658' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/cve-2026-57589' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenBSD has a use-after-free allowing local privilege escalation to root</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenBSD 存在使用后释放漏洞，可能本地提权至 root</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 06:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48831658' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 272</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48831658' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 145</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48831658'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenBSD fixes a use-after-free vulnerability that could allow a local user to escalate privileges to root. The flaw highlights the importance of memory-safety in OS kernels and will drive users to apply patches promptly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenBSD 发现一个使用后释放漏洞，可能使本地用户提权至 root。相关补丁即将发布，这再次凸显内存安全在内核中的重要性，促使运行中的系统尽快更新。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nvd.nist.gov/site-media/images/usbanner/us_flag_small.png' data-full='https://nvd.nist.gov/site-media/images/usbanner/us_flag_small.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48812055' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/small-language-models-ai-pharmaceuticals' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Small AI Models Gain Traction In places with unreliable networks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在网络不可靠的地区，小型 AI 模型获得关注</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 16:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48812055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 272</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48812055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48812055'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discusses how small AI models are gaining traction in environments with unreliable connectivity, enabling offline or low-bandwidth AI features. This shift improves privacy, resilience, and on-device capabilities for fieldwork and edge applications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍在网络不稳定地区，小型 AI 模型的实用性提升，能够离线或在低带宽环境中运行推断。此趋势提升了隐私性、鲁棒性，以及边缘设备的能力，适用于野外工作与现场应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67101133&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C889%2C0%2C361' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67101133&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C889%2C0%2C361' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.starringthecomputer.com/computers.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Starring the Computer</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48796093'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A thematic collection that treats computers as the star of technology history. It curates ideas and images that place computers at the center of innovation, offering engineers a curated lens on how hardware and software shaped each era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个以计算机为主角的主题集，整理了推动科技变革的历史脉络与视觉素材。为工程师提供一个聚焦硬件与软件如何共同推动各时代创新的视角。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cognition.com/blog/swe-1-7' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SWE-1.7 Reach Near GPT 5.5 and Opus Intelligence</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SWE-1.7 接近 GPT‑5.5 与 Opus Intelligence</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 09:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48833866' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 270</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48833866' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 139</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48833866'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SWE-1.7 pushes software-engineering AI features toward parity with GPT-5.5 and the Opus Intelligence framework, signaling how AI copilots may soon assist developers more deeply. The discussion covers implications for coding workflows, tool integration, and how teams should architect AI-powered systems. Expect faster prototyping and smarter automation as these capabilities mature.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SWE-1.7 的进展让软件工程领域的 AI 辅助能力接近 GPT-5.5 水平与 Opus Intelligence 框架的表现，意味着 AI 助手在编码工作流中的作用将更深。文章探讨了对代码工作流、工具整合以及构建 AI 驱动系统的影响。随着能力成熟，原型设计和自动化水平将显著提升。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48879504' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dakra.github.io/ghostel/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ghostel.el: Terminal emulator powered by libghostty</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ghostel.el：由 libghostty 驱动的终端模拟器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 12, 2026 / 01:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48879504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 270</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48879504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48879504'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ghostel.el demonstrates a terminal emulator built inside Emacs powered by libghostty, aiming for fast, responsive performance and solid VT support. It shows how a native library can supercharge a Lisp environment without leaving the editor. For power users, it represents a compelling option for integrated terminal workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ghostel.el 展示了在 Emacs 内部、利用 libghostty 驱动的终端模拟器，目标是实现快速、高响应和完善的 VT 支持。它展示了原生库如何在不离开编辑器的前提下提升 Lisp 环境的性能。对于重度用户，这是一个集成终端工作流的有力选择。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://melpa.org/packages/ghostel-badge.svg' data-full='https://melpa.org/packages/ghostel-badge.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48858292' data-tags='Politics,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48858292' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ec.europa.eu/commission/presscorner/home/en' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU Commission: addictive design Instagram and Facebook in breach of the DSA</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟委员会：Instagram 与 Facebook 的成瘾设计违反 DSA</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 04:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48858292' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 265</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48858292' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 184</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48858292'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The European Commission finds addictive design elements in Instagram and Facebook to breach the Digital Services Act, signaling a regulatory push against design choices that encourage prolonged use. The decision could push product teams to rethink engagement tactics and algorithmic incentives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟委员会认定 Instagram 与 Facebook 的成瘾性设计违反《数字服务法》，标志着对促成长期使用的设计做法的监管加强。此举可能促使产品团队重新审视黏性设计与算法激励策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ec.europa.eu/commission/presscorner/assets/framework/images/logo/ec_logo.png' data-full='https://ec.europa.eu/commission/presscorner/assets/framework/images/logo/ec_logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48873424' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://studyfinds.com/modern-decor-may-be-straining-peoples-brains/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Modern decor may be straining people&#x27;s brains</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>现代装饰可能增加大脑负担</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 09:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48873424' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 258</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48873424' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 252</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48873424'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cites studies suggesting modern decor increases cognitive load and reduces focus, with implications for workplace design and user interfaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示某些现代装饰风格会增加认知负荷、降低专注度，对工作场所设计和用户界面有启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://studyfinds.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Modern-office-scaled.jpeg' data-full='https://studyfinds.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Modern-office-scaled.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48862365' data-tags='Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48862365' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vfxblog.com/2017/08/23/the-tech-of-terminator-2-an-oral-history/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The tech of &#x27;Terminator 2&#x27; – an oral history (2017)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《终结者2》技术：口述史（2017）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 09:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48862365' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 257</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48862365' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48862365'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An oral history revisits the technical feats behind Terminator 2, highlighting practical effects, model work, and the early balance between analog and digital tricks. It shows how teams solved filmic challenges with ingenuity under tight budgets and time pressures. The piece illuminates a pivotal moment in visual effects history.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这部《终结者2》技术口述史回顾了特效背后的硬件与软件成就，强调实拍特效、模型工作以及早期的数字与模拟技术的结合。作者揭示了团队在预算和时间压力下如何凭借创造力解决难题，塑造了影像特效的历史转折点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://vfxblog.com/wp-content/uploads/2017/08/death1.jpg' data-full='https://vfxblog.com/wp-content/uploads/2017/08/death1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues why a new Postgres connection pooler is warranted, outlining design goals like low latency, reliability, and observability for cloud-native deployments. It explains where existing solutions fall short and how the new approach fits real workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阐释为何需要新一代 PostgreSQL 连接池，强调低延迟、可靠性与可观测性等设计目标，以及现有方案的局限，及新实现如何更好地适配云原生工作负载。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/meerkat-introduction/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloudflare Meerkat - Globally distributed consensus</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cloudflare Meerkat：全球分布式共识</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 06:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48831565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 252</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48831565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48831565'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meerkat introduces a globally distributed consensus mechanism to Cloudflare’s edge network, enabling more reliable coordination across data centers and endpoints. The approach aims to reduce latency and improve availability by bringing consensus closer to users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meerkat 为 Cloudflare 的边缘网络引入全球分布式共识机制，使数据中心与终端之间的协作更为可靠。其目标是将共识下放到更接近用户的层面，以降低延迟并提升可用性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ciphercue.com/blog/european-web-hosting-vendor-share-2026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Europe&#x27;s company websites are mostly served by US vendors</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 05:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48816612' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 250</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48816612' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 183</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48816612'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A sizable share of European company websites are hosted or served by US-based vendors, raising questions about data locality and cross-border surveillance concerns. The trend underscores dependence on US infrastructure for web delivery, with implications for privacy, security, and regulatory compliance within Europe.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲相当大比例的公司网站由美国供应商托管或提供服务，带来数据本地化与跨境监控的担忧。这一趋势凸显了对美国基础设施的依赖，对隐私、安全和欧洲范围内的合规治理具有重要影响。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 02, 2026 / 13:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48767058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 246</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48767058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 419</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48767058'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zuckerberg acknowledges that AI agent development is proceeding more slowly than hoped, citing technical and safety challenges. The admission tempers hype around near-term autonomous tools and underscores the need for thoughtful deployment, governance, and compute investment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>扎克伯格承认 AI 代理开发进展慢于预期，指出技术与安全方面的挑战。这一表态为市场对近期开源、自主代理工具的乐观预期降温，也提示需要更稳健的治理与算力投入。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post lays out how Anthropic&#x27;s public communications and strategic choices risk eroding goodwill among researchers, partners, and users. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出Anthropic在公开沟通和战略选择中可能侵蚀研究者、伙伴与用户的信任。论点认为对AI安全的夸大、与现实不符的宣传可能适得其反，并为在透明度、风险与投资者期望之间寻求平衡的初创公司提供教训。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pangram.com/blog/ai-in-your-feed' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI content is everywhere on social media, especially LinkedIn</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>社交媒体上的 AI 内容无处不在，LinkedIn 尤为明显</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 09, 2026 / 08:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48847940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 243</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48847940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 215</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48847940'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI-generated content is proliferating across social media, with LinkedIn showing a particularly high share. This trend accelerates content production for professionals but also challenges readers to assess authenticity and credibility. Platform policies and signal reliability will be key to preserving trust as feeds become more automated.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 生成的内容正在社交媒体中迅速蔓延，LinkedIn 的表现尤为突出。这一趋势提高了专业内容的产出效率，同时也让读者在辨别真实性和可信度方面面临挑战。平台的信号质量与监管政策将决定在日益自动化的信息流中的信任水平。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/separating-signal-from-noise-coding-evaluations/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Separating signal from noise in coding evaluations</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在编码评估中分辨信号与噪声</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 14:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48837396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 238</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48837396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 93</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48837396'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>With coding evaluations proliferating in tech hiring, signal can get buried under noise. This piece advocates rigorous evaluation practices—multimetric scoring, blind reviews, and reproducible benchmarks—to measure real coding ability rather than test-taking tricks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在技术招聘中，编码评估常被大量测试淹没信号。文章主张通过多元指标、盲评和可复现的基准来提炼有用信息，聚焦真实的编程能力，而非考试技巧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/5f0Xf4wOuXVsX7TZUM4tjl/4672bf7bf93bad67ade28dce57f62638/Seperating-signal-from-noise-SEO.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/5f0Xf4wOuXVsX7TZUM4tjl/4672bf7bf93bad67ade28dce57f62638/Seperating-signal-from-noise-SEO.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48859678' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ianreppel.org/how-successful-companies-go-blind/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Successful companies go blind</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>成功的公司会变得盲目</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 06:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48859678' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 238</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48859678' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48859678'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As companies scale, they can lose sight of fundamentals like customer needs and product quality. The article argues for deliberate checks—maintaining a &#x27;healthy paranoia&#x27; about risk, investing in reliability, and keeping feedback loops intact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>企业在快速成长后容易忽视基本面，如客户需求和产品质量。文章提倡保持对风险的健康警觉、加强可靠性建设，并维护有效的反馈循环。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48806289' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theargumentmag.com/p/should-dayquil-be-legal' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Should DayQuil Be Legal?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DayQuil 应否合法化？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 08:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48806289' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 236</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48806289' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 290</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48806289'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece questions whether DayQuil should face tighter controls or outright illegality, using OTC medicines to illustrate how policy might balance access with harm reduction. It advocates data-driven, pragmatic regulation rather than moral panic.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过日常感冒药 DayQuil 的案例，讨论药品监管的边界与公共卫生取舍。强调以数据驱动、务实的监管优于道德绑架，平衡可及性与滥用风险。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48862252' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48862252' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smithsonianmag.com/smart-news/spider-silk-loses-top-spot-natures-strongest-material-snails-teeth-180954346/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Snails&#x27; teeth beats spider silk as nature&#x27;s strongest material (2015)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蜗牛牙齿成自然界最强材料，超越蜘蛛丝（2015）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 09:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48862252' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 235</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48862252' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 166</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48862252'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2015 study found that snail teeth are among the strongest natural materials, outmatching spider silk due to their mineralized structure. This reshapes assumptions about how biology builds tough materials and inspires bio-inspired engineering approaches. The finding continues to influence materials science discussions today.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2015年的研究发现，蜗牛牙齿因矿化结构而具备极高强度，甚至超过蜘蛛丝。这一发现改变了对自然材料强度的直觉，并为生物启发式材料设计提供新思路，成为材料科学讨论的经典案例。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/uJ3_0X-O9Zw4wRJ-4uPA6-8sY-8=/fit-in/1600x0/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer/b6/2c/b62c97f3-8cc5-4351-9f3d-1641f48255cf/42-33773725.jpg' data-full='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/uJ3_0X-O9Zw4wRJ-4uPA6-8sY-8=/fit-in/1600x0/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer/b6/2c/b62c97f3-8cc5-4351-9f3d-1641f48255cf/42-33773725.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 04, 2026 / 09:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786789' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 234</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786789' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 134</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48786789'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Montreal&#x27;s watchmaking school marks 80 years of training craftsmen, balancing tradition with modern tooling. The profile highlights how the program adapts to a changing luxury and manufacturing landscape.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加拿大唯一的制表学校在80周年之际仍在教学，一方面传承工艺，另一方面适应现代工具与市场需求。报道展示学校如何在传统与现代之间找到平衡，维持这一小众领域的传承。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48872874' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://clickhouse.com/blog/pgbouncer-clickhouse-managed-postgres' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We scaled PgBouncer to 4x throughput</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PgBouncer 吞吐量提升四倍</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 08:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48872874' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 228</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48872874' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48872874'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shows how to push PgBouncer-based connection pooling to four times the throughput for PostgreSQL workloads, with practical tuning tips and pooling-mode considerations. Demonstrates integration with a managed Postgres stack to reduce connection overhead and latency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示如何将 PgBouncer 基于连接池的吞吐量提升至四倍，给出实际的参数调整和池模式选择要点。展示在 ClickHouse 管理的 Postgres 场景中降低连接开销、提升延迟表现的做法。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48881874' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48881874' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(134) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/leaders/2026/07/09/a-no-brainer-for-protecting-your-brain' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The shingles vaccine may reduce the risk of dementia</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>带状疱疹疫苗或降低痴呆风险</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 12, 2026 / 08:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48881874' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48881874' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 187</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48881874'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Emerging evidence links shingles vaccination to a lower risk of developing dementia later in life, though researchers caution that correlation does not prove causation. If replicated, this could influence public health messaging and encourage older adults to complete the vaccine schedule. Researchers urge careful interpretation due to potential confounders.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>初步证据显示带状疱疹疫苗与晚期痴呆风险降低相关，但相关性不等于因果关系。若结果被重复验证，可能影响公共卫生信息并鼓励老年人按时接种疫苗。研究者提醒需警惕混杂因素的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48863707' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48863707' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://casp.ac/reports/ai-enabled-terrorism' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How the terrorist group Boko Haram uses frontier AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>恐怖组织博科圣地如何利用前沿人工智能</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 11:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48863707' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48863707' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 201</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48863707'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A CASP report documents how Boko Haram leverages frontier AI to plan and execute operations, illustrating the evolving threat of AI-enabled extremism. The findings underscore the need for monitoring, safeguards, and countermeasures to prevent misuse. It also highlights the broader risk landscape as AI technologies spread.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份 CASP 报告揭示博科圣地利用前沿 AI 进行作战计划与行动，体现了 AI 助力极端主义的新演变。研究强调需要加强监控、建立防护措施并制定对策以防止滥用，同时也反映出随着 AI 技术普及，风险格局正在扩大。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gpt-engineer-file-uploads/AwmrFUJUl1Miu5dNMpFBx8P372K3/social-images/social-1783516958074-CASP_Social_Logos_White.webp' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gpt-engineer-file-uploads/AwmrFUJUl1Miu5dNMpFBx8P372K3/social-images/social-1783516958074-CASP_Social_Logos_White.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48823981' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48823981' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thebeach.dev/posts/lisp-agent/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An agent in 100 lines of Lisp</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 100 行 Lisp 实现的智能体</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 14:21 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-title'>(137) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://avi.press/posts/2026-07-10-after-7-years-in-production-scarf-has-reluctantly-moved-away-from-haskell.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>After 7 years in production, Scarf has reluctantly moved away from Haskell</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 06:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48859673' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 226</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48859673' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 286</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48859673'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>After seven years in production, the Scarf project has reluctantly moved away from Haskell, citing reasons like ecosystem maturity, performance, or maintenance constraints. The decision reflects the real-world tradeoffs teams face when choosing a language for long-running systems software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>经过七年的生产实践，Scarf 项目被迫放弃在生产环境中使用 Haskell，原因包括生态系统成熟度、性能与维护成本等权衡。这反映出长期运行的系统在语言选择上的现实考量。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 05, 2026 / 14:32 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Eight years into development, Homegames is an open-source game platform designed for modular, community-driven extension. It demonstrates how a long-running indie project can mature into a usable platform despite sustainability challenges, with architecture that invites contributions from hobbyists and developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>经过8年的开发，Homegames 成为一个面向模块化扩展、由社区驱动的开源游戏平台。该项目展示了如何让一个长期的独立项目逐步成熟为可用平台，同时也暴露出维护与持续性的挑战，以及激励社区贡献的架构设计。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48813929' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(139) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www-cs-faculty.stanford.edu/~knuth/taocp.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Art of Computer Programming by Donald E. Knuth</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>唐纳德·E·克努斯的《计算机程序设计艺术》</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 22:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48813929' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 224</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48813929' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48813929'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Donald E. Knuth&#x27;s The Art of Computer Programming remains the gold standard for rigorous algorithm analysis and software correctness. It blends deep theory with meticulous typesetting, and is still evolving with new volumes and exercises, influencing generations of computer scientists and programmers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《计算机程序设计艺术》仍是严格算法分析与软件正确性领域的权威之作，理论深邃、排版考究，且通过新增卷本和习题继续演进，深刻影响着新一代计算机科学家与程序员。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www-cs-faculty.stanford.edu/~knuth/1234ab.jpg' data-full='https://www-cs-faculty.stanford.edu/~knuth/1234ab.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48870381' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(140) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mixfont.com/ghost-font' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ghost Font: A font that humans can read but AI cannot</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>幽灵字体：人眼可读、AI不可读的字体</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 02:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48870381' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 223</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48870381' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 164</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48870381'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ghost Font is designed so humans can read the text while machines struggle to interpret it. It highlights a tension between human readability and machine parsing, with implications for data scraping, copyright enforcement, and model training datasets. The idea could spur new anti-scraping techniques, even as OCR continues to improve.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>幽灵字体让人眼可读、机器难以识别，折射出人机可读性与机器解析之间的张力。对数据抓取、版权保护和模型训练数据集具有潜在影响，同时也暴露出 OCR 技术的对抗性与演进空间。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.mixfont.com/assets/20260711-092742-ghostfont-og-j3qj8h8p.webp' data-full='https://static.mixfont.com/assets/20260711-092742-ghostfont-og-j3qj8h8p.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(143) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://clojure.org/news/2026/07/02/clojure-1-13-alpha1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Clojure 1.13 adds support for checked keys</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Clojure 1.13 增加对检查键的支持</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 02, 2026 / 13:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48767211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 217</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48767211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48767211'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Clojure 1.13 adds support for checked keys, enabling safer map lookups and earlier error reporting in dynamic data structures. This reduces silent failures in complex apps and improves debugging.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Clojure 1.13 增加对检查键的支持，使映射查找更安全、错误报告更早，对复杂应用中的潜在静默失败有帮助，提升调试效率。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48837162' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 214</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48837162' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 189</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48837162'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic&#x27;s safety classifiers used for Fable are criticized as being overly aggressive, making the model less useful in practice. The critique argues for more nuanced alignment to avoid blocking benign content while still guarding against harm.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于 Anthropic 为 Fable 设置的安全分类器，被批评过于严苛，削弱了模型的实用性。该评议呼吁在防护与可用性之间取得更细致的对齐，避免误封有害内容。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://combine-lab.github.io/icons/share-thumbnail.jpg' data-full='https://combine-lab.github.io/icons/share-thumbnail.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/iOfficeAI/OfficeCLI' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OfficeCLI: Office suite for AI agents to read and edit Microsoft Office files</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OfficeCLI：供 AI 代理读取与编辑 Office 文件的办公套件</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 09:47 PT</span></p>

It enables automation workflows where language models manipulate documents as part of larger tasks. The project signals growing tooling for AI-driven document automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OfficeCLI 提供一个命令行界面，使 AI 代理能够读取、解析并编辑 Word、Excel 和 PowerPoint 文件。它支持将语言模型的推理结果整合到文档自动化工作流中。该项目体现了 AI 驱动的文档自动化工具链的日益成熟。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danielstanica.com/posts/Great-Blogging-Collapse' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The great blogging collapse: What happened to 100 successful blogs?</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 02, 2026 / 02:36 PT</span></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48758802'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis of why many once-successful blogs faded, pointing to platform shifts, audience fragmentation, monetization limits, and the rise of social media. It offers takeaways on sustainable blogging or archiving.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析了曾经成功的博客大量消退的原因，指出平台演变、读者分散、盈利受限以及社媒崛起等因素，并给出可持续博客模式的启示。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://astro.build/blog/astro-7/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Astro 7.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Astro 7.0</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 11:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48821653' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 210</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48821653' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48821653'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astro 7.0 lands with a major update to the popular web framework, focusing on faster builds, improved rendering, and a streamlined DX for developers building fast, content-heavy sites. The release tightens integration with modern frontend tooling while preserving Astro&#x27;s component islands approach and performance-first philosophy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Astro 7.0 发布，带来对这款受欢迎的 Web 框架的重大更新，聚焦更高的构建速度、改进的渲染和更顺畅的开发体验，适用于构建高性能的内容密集型站点。新版本延续“岛屿架构”并增强与现代前端工具的集成。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48795900' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://combustionlab.net' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Combustion engine web-based simulator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>基于网页的燃烧引擎模拟器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 05, 2026 / 10:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48795900' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 208</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48795900' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48795900'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A web-based simulator lets learners explore combustion engine behavior directly in the browser, enabling hands-on thermodynamics experiments without hardware. It’s a useful educational tool for students and hobbyists to test concepts and ideas quickly. The project lowers barriers to understanding engine dynamics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个基于网页的模拟器允许学习者直接在浏览器中研究燃烧发动机的行为，免去硬件需求，便于做热力学的动手实验。对学生和爱好者来说，这是一个快速测试概念与想法的有用教育工具。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48868996' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48868996' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://designyoutrust.com/2018/01/vintage-beauty-soviet-control-rooms/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The vintage beauty of Soviet control rooms (2018)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苏联控制室的复古之美（2018）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 22:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48868996' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 208</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48868996' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48868996'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>These rooms showcase vast, modular consoles, glowing CRT displays, and tactile controls that reflect Soviet-era computing and operational culture. The imagery highlights a design language where humans and machines coexisted in the control room, a contrast to today’s lean dashboards. The piece is a reminder that industrial aesthetics can influence contemporary UI/UX design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这些照片展示了广阔的模组化控制台、发光的 CRT 显示和大量触控旋钮，映射出苏联时代的计算与运维文化。其设计语言强调人机共处的工作场景，与今日的简洁仪表板形成对照。该组影像提醒人们，工业美学仍能影响现代 UI/UX 设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://designyoutrust.com/wp-content/uploads/2018/01/1-17.jpg' data-full='https://designyoutrust.com/wp-content/uploads/2018/01/1-17.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48874200' data-tags='Culture,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48874200' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://geohot.github.io//blog/jekyll/update/2026/07/11/ai-2040.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI 2040 and the cult of intelligence</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 2040与智能崇拜</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 11:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48874200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 207</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48874200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 249</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48874200'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A thoughtful piece examining how hype around AI 2040 can become a cultural religion, shaping policy and investment more than actual capabilities. It critiques techno-optimism and urges critical thinking about what &#x27;intelligence&#x27; really means in AI, and how societies prepare for it.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过分析AI在2040年前后的热潮如何演变为一种文化信仰，文章指出所宣称的能力与现实之间的差距可能左右政策和投资。批评技术乐观主义，呼吁对“智能”在AI中的含义以及社会应对策略保持批判性思考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://geohot.github.io/blog/assets/images/ocean_datacenter.jpg' data-full='https://geohot.github.io/blog/assets/images/ocean_datacenter.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48805598' data-tags='AI,Hardware,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48805598' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.macrumors.com/2026/07/06/apple-silicon-exec-explains-mac-mini-ai-demand/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple Silicon Exec Explains Mac Mini AI Demand and On-Device Future</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果芯片高管解读 Mac mini 的 AI 需求与设备端未来</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 07:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48805598' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 203</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48805598' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 292</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48805598'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An Apple Silicon executive explains that AI workloads are driving demand for the Mac mini and that Apple is prioritizing on-device AI to improve privacy and latency. The note suggests the Mac mini could serve as a low-cost testbed for developers and as a stepping stone to a broader on-device AI strategy across Apple devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果芯片高管表示，AI 工作负载推动 Mac mini 的需求增长，苹果将优先发展设备端推理以提升隐私与响应速度。Mac mini 可能成为开发者的低成本测试平台，并作为苹果在全系设备上推进设备端 AI 策略的一个起点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.macrumors.com/t/J46PnqFon6uZq7AZIAThCrBf36g=/2500x/article-new/2024/04/apple-silicon-feature-joeblue.jpg' data-full='https://images.macrumors.com/t/J46PnqFon6uZq7AZIAThCrBf36g=/2500x/article-new/2024/04/apple-silicon-feature-joeblue.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48798815' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48798815' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20049' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Does code cleanliness affect coding agents? A controlled minimal-pair study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代码清洁度会影响编程代理吗？一项受控的最小对比研究</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 05, 2026 / 16:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48798815' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 201</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48798815' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 93</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48798815'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A controlled minimal-pair study investigates whether code cleanliness affects coding agents. The results suggest cleaner, more readable code helps agents produce more reliable outputs, with implications for prompt design and when outsourcing coding tasks to AI. It argues readability isn&#x27;t just a human virtue; it can guide AI tooling too.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项受控的最小对比研究发现，代码的可读性确实会影响编码代理的表现，整洁的代码能让代理产出更可靠的结果。这意味着在进行 AI 辅助编码时，仍应重视代码结构与可维护性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48803707' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48803707' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dailymail.com/sciencetech/article-15956159/Incredible-lost-city-discovered-Egypts-desert.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lost city discovered beneath Egypt&#x27;s desert with ancient church</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>埃及沙漠下发现失落城市与古教堂</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 05:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48803707' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 200</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48803707' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 180</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48803707'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Archaeologists have uncovered a previously unknown city buried beneath the Egyptian desert, along with an ancient church. The find sheds light on urban life and early Christian presence in the region, illustrating how desert geology can preserve antiquities for study. It also hints at trade routes and cultural exchange in ancient Egypt.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>考古学家在埃及沙漠下发现一座未知的失落城市，以及一座古老教堂。此发现揭示了该地区早期城市生活与基督教早期足迹，说明沙漠地质对文物保存的重要性，同时也指向古代埃及的贸易网络与文化交流。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48804014' data-tags='DevOps,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48804014' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/workers-cache/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Workers Cache</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cloudflare Workers 缓存机制</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 06:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48804014' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 197</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48804014' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48804014'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare introduces new edge caching primitives for Workers, enabling faster responses and lower costs by caching dynamic content at the edge. This empowers developers to boost performance for latency-sensitive apps and improve resilience during outages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 为 Workers 引入新的边缘缓存原语，使动态内容可在离用户最近的边缘缓存，从而提升响应速度、降低成本。开发者因此可以为时延敏感的应用提升性能，并在网络故障时提高容错性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/6S1TZg4kL1049Rkw44RiIc/5adca4df8432fb3401517712f2b8d15f/Your_Worker_can_now_have_its_own_cache_in_front_of_it-OG.png' data-full='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/6S1TZg4kL1049Rkw44RiIc/5adca4df8432fb3401517712f2b8d15f/Your_Worker_can_now_have_its_own_cache_in_front_of_it-OG.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
