<p class='hn-title'>(161) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Wren6991/RISCBoy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RISCBoy is an open-source portable games console, designed from scratch</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 14:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48876245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 196</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48876245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48876245'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RISCBoy is an open-source, portable game console built from scratch, likely leveraging a RISC-V core. It showcases a DIY hardware ethos and offers a community-driven alternative to proprietary handhelds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RISCBoy 是一个开源、便携的游戏机，完全从零设计，可能基于 RISC-V 架构。它体现了动手做硬件的精神，以及对专有掌上设备的社区驱动替代方案的探索。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Preview showcases ultra-small 8-bit emulators, designed for web and embedded contexts with minimal code. The project aims to deliver authentic retro computing experiences on tiny runtimes, enabling hobbyists to run classic software on constrained devices. It demonstrates how compact emulation can broaden access to retro computing in modern tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该预览展示了极小型的 8 位仿真器，代码量极小，适合在网页或嵌入式环境中运行。项目旨在让在受限设备上也能体验经典的“复古计算”，为爱好者提供运行老软件与工具的可能。该工作体现了极小体量仿真在现代工具链中的应用潜力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(164) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.publicnotice.co/p/kalshi-cnn-cnbc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Kalshi Infects the News</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 06:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48804123' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 194</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48804123' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 187</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Predictive markets like Kalshi can influence what gets reported by attaching real-money bets to news events, creating feedback loops between journalism and markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>预测市场把现实金钱赌注押在新闻事件上，可能塑造报道议程并与市场形成互相反馈。分析对媒体独立性、问责性以及信息生态的监管风险影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nahla.dev/blog/waitfree_queue/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Girls just wanna have fast MPMC queues with bounded waiting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>带有界等待的高速 MPMC 队列</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 12:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48809574' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 193</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48809574' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48809574'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Research presents fast MPMC queues with bounded waiting, addressing a key bottleneck in concurrent data structures. The work offers practical approaches to reduce latency in multi-threaded systems, with potential impact on high-performance services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究提出带有界等待的高速多生产者多消费者队列，解决并发数据结构的关键瓶颈。此工作提供降低多线程延迟的实际方法，可能影响高性能服务的性能边界。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48834898' data-tags='Science,Health,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48834898' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://e360.yale.edu/features/cassandra-rauert-interview' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What Do We Know About the Microplastics Inside Us?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们体内的微塑料到底有多少？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 10:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48834898' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 192</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48834898' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48834898'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New reporting highlights detectable microplastics in human tissues, sourced from food and environment, with uncertain long-term health effects. The piece underscores pervasive exposure and the need for further research, stronger regulation, and safer product design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新报道揭示人体组织中可检测的微塑料来自食物与环境，长期健康影响尚不明确。文章强调暴露普遍性与需要进一步研究、加强监管和更安全的产品设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://yale-threesixty.transforms.svdcdn.com/production/Plastic-Fingertips_Getty-HEADER.jpg?w=1200&amp;q=60&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;dm=1783439743&amp;s=6ae88795970a9e9a134849ba859c0c1a' data-full='https://yale-threesixty.transforms.svdcdn.com/production/Plastic-Fingertips_Getty-HEADER.jpg?w=1200&amp;q=60&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;dm=1783439743&amp;s=6ae88795970a9e9a134849ba859c0c1a' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48837110' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48837110' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/linuxrebel/DocuBrowser' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Turning a pile of documents into a searchable useable knowledge base</a></p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://waratlas.org' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>War Atlas: An interactive cartography of every named war in human history</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>战争地图集：互动呈现人类历史上的所有命名战争</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 10:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48863080' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 183</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48863080' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48863080'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>War Atlas offers an interactive map of named wars across history, letting users explore when and where conflicts happened and how large they were. The visualization helps researchers, educators, and curious readers grasp the scale and geography of conflict over time. It’s a compelling data-driven lens on world history.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>War Atlas 提供跨历史的命名战争互动地图，便于用户查看战争发生的时间、地点及规模。该可视化工具对研究者、教育工作者与好奇读者理解冲突的时空分布具有帮助，提供了以数据驱动的历史视角。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(178) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.yaelwrites.com/stop-telling-me-to-ask-an-llm/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stop Telling Me to Ask an LLM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>别再让我去问大型语言模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 15:28 PT</span></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48876441'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Author argues against outsourcing thinking to LLMs, highlighting risks of misinformation, context loss, and misaligned incentives. Proposes strategies to combine human judgment with AI tools, preserving agency and critical thinking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者反对把思考交给LLM，指出错误信息、上下文丢失和激励错位的风险。提出将人类判断与AI工具结合的策略，维护自主性与批判性思维。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 09, 2026 / 07:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48846995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 180</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48846995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 145</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48846995'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Best practices for issuing and rotating TLS certificates for internal services, emphasizing automation, short-lived certs, and mTLS between services. Proper internal PKI reduces risk and improves security for service-to-service communication.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>内部服务 TLS 证书的最佳实践，聚焦自动化、短期证书和服务间 mTLS。健全的内部公钥基础设施能降低横向渗透风险，提升微服务间通信的安全性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48796817' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://intextbooks.science.uu.nl/workshop2026/files/itb26_s1s2.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New AI tutor achieves 0.71-1.30 SD effect size in Dartmouth course [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>达特茅斯课程中的新AI辅导取得0.71–1.30标准差的效应量</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 05, 2026 / 11:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48796817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 178</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48796817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 112</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48796817'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new AI tutoring system delivered measurable gains in a Dartmouth course, achieving an effect size in the 0.71–1.30 SD range. This demonstrates AI tutors&#x27; potential to scale personalized help, though results may vary by subject, and practical deployment raises equity and privacy considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个新的AI辅导系统在达特茅斯的一门课程中实现了可衡量的学习提升，效应量落在0.71到1.30标准差区间。这表明AI辅导在大规模提供个性化帮助方面具有潜力，但不同学科差异、以及实际部署中的公平性与隐私问题需要关注。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48805401' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48805401' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/stochastic-parrot' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What Emily Bender meant by &quot;stochastic parrots&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>埃米莉·本德所指的随机鹦鹉</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 07:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48805401' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48805401' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 256</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48805401'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains Emily Bender&#x27;s stochastic parrots concept—LLMs imitate human text by statistical patterns, not understanding. Highlights concerns about biases, environmental cost, and misaligned incentives, urging better evaluation and governance of AI systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>解释“随机鹦鹀”概念：语言模型通过统计模式模仿文本，而非真正理解。强调偏见、环境成本与激励错位等问题，呼吁对 AI 系统进行更严格的评估与治理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/q-a-emily-bender-on-stochastic-parrots-5-years-later.jpg?id=67050137&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C16%2C0%2C234' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/q-a-emily-bender-on-stochastic-parrots-5-years-later.jpg?id=67050137&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C16%2C0%2C234' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48834309' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48834309' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nebusec.ai/research/ionstack-part-2/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GhostLock, a stack-UAF that has existed in all Linux distributions for 15 years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GhostLock：已在所有 Linux 发行版中存在 15 年的栈上使用后释放漏洞</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 09:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48834309' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48834309' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48834309'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GhostLock is a stack-use-after-free vulnerability that has persisted across every Linux distribution for 15 years. Its ubiquity makes patching difficult and raises questions about how many systems remain unpatched, underscoring the need for systemic mitigations and kernel-level hardening.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GhostLock 是一个栈上使用后释放漏洞，已在所有 Linux 发行版中存在长达 15 年。问题的广泛性使修补工作复杂且难以全面覆盖，凸显对内核层面对策和系统级强化的迫切需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nebusec.ai/_astro/social-preview.BvQ1tugL.png' data-full='https://nebusec.ai/_astro/social-preview.BvQ1tugL.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(185) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tryai.dev/blog/grok-4.5-vs-gpt-5.5-vs-claude-build-off' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We made Grok 4.5, GPT-5.5, and Claude build the same apps</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们让 Grok 4.5、GPT‑5.5 与 Claude 一起构建同样的应用</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 16:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48838772' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 172</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48838772' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48838772'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A build-off comparing Grok 4.5, GPT-5.5, and Claude shows that all three models can independently produce the same apps, illustrating cross-model parity and the practical limits of AI-assisted development. The results help set expectations for developers using multi-LLM toolchains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对比测试表明，Grok 4.5、GPT‑5.5 与 Claude 都能独立构建出相同的应用，显示出跨模型的能力接近及 AI 辅助开发的现实边界。结果为使用多模型工具链的开发者设定了期望值。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将生产级 AI 代理迁移至 GPT-5.6：速度提升 2.2 倍、成本降低 27%</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882716' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 172</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882716' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A production-grade case study migrating an AI agent to GPT-5.6, delivering about 2.2x throughput and 27% cost savings. The write-up covers integration tweaks, latency considerations, and risk controls when upgrading models in production, offering practical guidance for teams planning a refresh.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇案例研究记录了把生产环境中的 AI 代理迁移到 GPT-5.6 的过程，吞吐量提升约 2.2 倍、成本下降约 27%。文末给出在生产环境升级模型时的集成优化、延迟考量和风险控制要点，为计划升级的团队提供实用建议。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48854247' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/psychology/articles/10.3389/fpsyg.2026.1766665/full' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Parental device use and the adolescent-caregiver attachment bond</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>父母设备使用与青少年-照护者依恋关系</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 09, 2026 / 17:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48854247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48854247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 149</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48854247'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The study investigates how parental device usage during interactions relates to the adolescent-caregiver attachment bond, finding that higher screen usage is linked to weaker attachment quality and less responsive parenting. The findings highlight the fragility of the parent-child bond in the digital era and suggest strategies for mindful tech use at home.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究探讨了父母在互动中使用电子设备对青少年照护者依恋关系的影响，结果显示高屏幕使用与依恋质量下降和反应性下降相关。研究强调数字时代家庭关系的脆弱性，并提出在家庭中实行更有意识的科技使用的策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d2csxpduxe849s.cloudfront.net/media/E32629C6-9347-4F84-81FEAEF7BFA342B3/0F39389F-DE2D-4BF1-AFE71BA9B6F25024/918D96DB-F484-4EC1-8FBF9AB5F74B20F4/WebsiteWebP_XL-FPSYG_Main Visual_Green_Website.webp' data-full='https://d2csxpduxe849s.cloudfront.net/media/E32629C6-9347-4F84-81FEAEF7BFA342B3/0F39389F-DE2D-4BF1-AFE71BA9B6F25024/918D96DB-F484-4EC1-8FBF9AB5F74B20F4/WebsiteWebP_XL-FPSYG_Main Visual_Green_Website.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports a Delta flight being struck by a firework during approach to Chicago&#x27;s Midway, with no injuries; raises questions about crowd safety and airport perimeter controls around Independence Day celebrations.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www3.nhk.or.jp/nhkworld/en/news/20260705_01/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Japan&#x27;s Hayabusa2 probe to conduct flyby of Torifune asteroid</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本的 Hayabusa2 探测器将对 Torifune 小行星进行掠过</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 05, 2026 / 03:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48792980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48792980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48792980'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Japan&#x27;s Hayabusa2 probe will perform a flyby of the Torifune asteroid, expanding its reconnaissance of near-Earth objects. The mission builds on Hayabusa2&#x27;s heritage of sample collection, contributing data about asteroid composition and evolution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本的隼鸟2号探测器将对 Torifune 小行星进行掠过，继续对近地小行星进行探测。该任务延续了 Hayabusa2 的样本返回传统，为了解小行星组成和演化提供新数据。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48821378'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new runtime named &#x27;l&#x27; is introduced for k and q languages, expanding how analysts deploy and run kdb+-based workloads. The runtime promises streamlined execution, portability across platforms, and potential performance gains for time-series workloads. If adoption grows, it could simplify deploying analytics stacks in cloud and edge environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>&#x27;l&#x27; 作为针对 k 和 q 语言的新运行时推出，扩展了对 kdb+ 时序分析任务的执行方式。该运行时承诺实现更简洁的执行、跨平台可移植性，以及可能的性能提升，有助于在云端与边缘环境部署分析工作负载。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An in-depth Cambridge guide on preserving data from aging floppy disks. <p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 05, 2026 / 17:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48799256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 163</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48799256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48799256'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Al Vigier argues Canada should craft its AI strategy with transparency and public accountability, avoiding opaque deals with private contractors. The piece advocates open governance and alignment with national interests over hidden Palantir-style billing.</p>

The coverage underscores how mounting antitrust, privacy, and platform liability cases could reshape Meta&#x27;s business model, governance, and investor sentiment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 正面临可能累积达到万亿级别的庞大诉讼，构成对扎克伯格旗下公司的财政与监管压力。报道强调日益增多的反垄断、隐私和平台责任诉讼可能改变 Meta 的商业模式、治理结构与投资者情绪。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48800596' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nexte.st/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cargo-nextest: 3x faster than cargo test, per-test isolation, first-class CI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cargo-nextest：比 cargo test 快3倍、逐测隔离、CI 更好</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 05, 2026 / 21:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48800596' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 160</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48800596' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48800596'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cargo-nextest promises roughly a 3x speedup over cargo test by isolating tests at the per-test level. It preserves Rust compatibility while delivering faster CI feedback, benefiting large codebases and teams practicing test-driven workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cargo-nextest 通过逐测隔离实现对 cargo test 的约3x加速，提升大型代码库的CI反馈速度，同时保持与 Rust 工具链的兼容性。对推行测试驱动开发的团队尤其有利。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nexte.st/assets/images/social/index.png' data-full='https://nexte.st/assets/images/social/index.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48806410' data-tags='Programming,Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48806410' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kani: A Model Checker for Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kani：Rust 的模型检查器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 08:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48806410' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 160</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48806410' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48806410'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kani brings model checking to Rust, enabling exhaustive verification of safety properties beyond conventional tests. It integrates with Rust tooling and uses an LLVM backend to explore possible states, catching concurrency and memory-safety issues early. This lowers barriers to formal verification for systems programming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kani 将模型检查引入 Rust，能够对安全属性进行穷举验证，超越传统测试的覆盖面。它与 Rust 工具链集成，借助 LLVM 后端遍历潜在状态，提前发现并发与内存安全问题。这将降低系统编程中的形式化验证门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48878126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 160</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48878126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 341</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48878126'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A federal rule requires colleges to demonstrate that their graduates fare better financially or risk loss of federal financial aid eligibility. The shift signals tighter accountability for higher education outcomes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>联邦规定要求高校证明毕业生在经济上更有改善，否則将可能失去联邦资助资格。这一转变标志着对高校结果的更严格问责。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48865093' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48865093' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48865093'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Several AI models independently build the same four apps, illustrating rapid prototyping and convergent capabilities across toolkits. The trend highlights how developers can rely on multiple backends for similar outcomes, while raising questions about vendor lock-in, consistency, and maintenance across runtimes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>多套 AI 模型同时独立构建同样的四个应用，显示出快速原型迭代和能力收敛。趋势表明开发者可以依赖不同后端实现相同结果，但也带来供应商锁定、跨运行时的一致性与维护难题等挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(205) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/cosmtrek/mindwalk' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Mindwalk – Replay coding-agent sessions on a 3D map of your codebase</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Mindwalk——在代码库的3D地图上回放编码代理会话</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 22:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48878682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 151</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48878682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48878682'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mindwalk lets you replay coding-agent sessions on a 3D map of your codebase, helping you trace decisions and debugging. The visualization could accelerate understanding of model behavior across large repos and improve collaboration. This is especially useful for teams building AI-assisted coding tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mindwalk 允许在代码库的三维地图上回放编码代理会话，帮助追踪决策与调试。可视化有助于理解大型代码库中模型行为，提升协作效率，尤其对开发 AI 辅助编码工具的团队有明显帮助。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>软件质量笔记</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 11:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48821441' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 150</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48821441' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48821441'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A compact reflection on software quality, balancing speed with reliability and maintainability. It offers practical tips on testing, code hygiene, and design choices that improve long-term resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对软件质量的简短反思，强调在速度、可靠性与可维护性之间取得平衡，并给出测试、代码整洁和设计选择等实用建议以提升长期鲁棒性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48880499' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48880499' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(207) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://odinbook.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Understanding the Odin programming language</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>理解 Odin 编程语言</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 12, 2026 / 05:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48880499' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 149</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48880499' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48880499'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Odin is a small, pragmatic systems language designed for clarity and performance, with explicit memory management and a simple toolchain. It aims to give comparable control to C or Zig without some of their complexity, making it attractive for performance-critical tasks. This overview highlights where Odin shines and where it fits in the systems-programming landscape.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Odin 是一个简洁实用的系统级语言，强调清晰度与性能，具备显式内存管理和简化的工具链。它在避免某些复杂性的同时提供与 C/ Zig 相当的控制力，适合对性能敏感的任务。本文聚焦 Odin 的优势领域及其在系统编程中的定位。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://odinbook.com/og_img_new.jpg' data-full='https://odinbook.com/og_img_new.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48837696' data-tags='AI'>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 09:48 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Biff.graph lets you model a Clojure codebase as a graph that can be queried, enabling structural analysis and refactoring insights. It helps developers inspect dependencies, call graphs, and relationships across namespaces.</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide to trimming Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) context to only what the answer requires, reducing cost and latency without sacrificing quality. It covers relevance filtering, chunking, and dynamic context sizing to preserve answer fidelity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍在检索增强生成中，将上下文裁剪至回答所需的最小信息的实用方法，以降低成本与延迟，同时保持输出质量。内容涵盖相关性筛选、分块策略和动态上下文尺寸的做法。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(219) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.vt.edu/articles/2025/11/research_fralinbiomed_yanweightlifting.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Weightlifting beats running for blood sugar control, researchers find (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>研究发现举重对血糖控制优于跑步（2025）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 15:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48876593' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 138</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48876593' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48876593'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study finds weightlifting more effective than running for improving blood sugar control, possibly by increasing insulin sensitivity and muscle mass. The finding could reshape exercise prescriptions for diabetes and metabolic health, emphasizing resistance training.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现举重在改善血糖控制方面优于跑步，可能通过提升胰岛素敏感性和增加肌肉量实现。此发现或将改变糖尿病与代谢健康的运动处方，强调力量训练的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.vt.edu/content/news_vt_edu/en/articles/2025/11/research_fralinbiomed_yanweightlifting/_jcr_content/image.transform/l-medium/image.jpg' data-full='https://news.vt.edu/content/news_vt_edu/en/articles/2025/11/research_fralinbiomed_yanweightlifting/_jcr_content/image.transform/l-medium/image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(220) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://janilowski.pl/en/blog/2026/price-per-m-tokens/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Price per 1M tokens is meaningless</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>每百万 token 的价格毫无意义</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 12:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48809542' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 137</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48809542' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48809542'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Price per 1M tokens is a poor comparability metric across LLMs due to tokenization and usage differences. The piece recommends evaluating true costs per useful output and considering model efficiency and latency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>按每百万 token 的价格来比较语言模型不可取，因为分词方式和实际用法差异会扭曲结果。建议以有用输出的真实成本、模型效率与延迟等因素综合评估。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(229) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/on-prem/2026/07/11/irish-datacenters-now-guzzle-23-of-the-countrys-electricity/5270013' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Irish datacenters now guzzle 23% of the country&#x27;s electricity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>爱尔兰数据中心耗电占比达 23%</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 12, 2026 / 13:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48884322' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48884322' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48884322'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ireland’s datacenters now account for 23% of national electricity consumption, signaling the sector’s impact on energy demand and grid stability. The figure underscores energy intensity, growth in data infrastructure, and potential policy or efficiency measures to balance reliability with sustainability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>爱尔兰数据中心现占全国用电量的 23%，凸显该行业对电网与气候目标的压力。用电需求持续增长、能耗密集性显现，未来可能推动更高效运行、能源政策调整和区域化能源规划。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/4093897.jpg?imageId=4093897&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=95.56&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=95.56&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/4093897.jpg?imageId=4093897&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=95.56&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=95.56&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48861923' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48861923' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(230) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://softwaredoug.com/blog/2026/07/09/write-code' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why write code in 2026</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么在 2026 年写代码</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 09:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48861923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48861923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 170</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48861923'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>By 2026, coding is shifting from typing boilerplate to composing with AI-assisted tools and high-level abstractions. The piece argues that success will hinge on architectural thinking, system design, and effective collaboration, not just syntax. It offers pragmatic tips for adapting to this evolving workflow.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>到 2026 年，编码将从大量重复性敲击转向使用 AI 辅助工具和高层抽象进行组合。文章认为成功取决于架构思维、系统设计和高效协作，而不仅仅是语法。并给出适应这一演变工作流的实用建议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://softwaredoug.com//assets/media/2026/write-code/image1.png' data-full='https://softwaredoug.com//assets/media/2026/write-code/image1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48874357' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48874357' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(231) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/science-and-technology/2026/07/08/how-to-hide-from-killer-drones' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to hide from killer drones</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何躲避致命无人机</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 11:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48874357' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48874357' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 164</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48874357'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece outlines practical and ethical considerations for counter-drone strategies, from concealment to shielding, while noting limits and risks of civilian safety. The broader takeaway: as drone threat grows, defense research raises questions about privacy and civil liberties.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章概述对抗无人机的策略要点，包括隐蔽、屏蔽等方法及相关伦理与安全边界，强调民用安全的局限性。随着无人机威胁上升，防御研究带来的将是隐私与公民自由的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48835439' data-tags='Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48835439' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(232) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://forum.openmandriva.org/t/statement-regarding-attempted-distribution-sabotage/8997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenMandriva: Statement regarding attempted distribution sabotage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenMandriva：关于试图破坏发行版的声明</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 11:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48835439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48835439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48835439'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenMandriva releases a statement after an attempted sabotage of its distribution, detailing security measures and steps to protect users. The incident highlights ongoing supply-chain and distribution risks for open-source projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenMandriva 在发行版试图被破坏后发布声明，阐述了安全措施以及对用户的保护步骤。此事件凸显开源项目在供应链与发行环节面临的持续风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://forum.openmandriva.org/uploads/default/original/2X/e/eb6bc6e5e60da2e909cb97a086c16d0a7c130855.jpeg' data-full='https://forum.openmandriva.org/uploads/default/original/2X/e/eb6bc6e5e60da2e909cb97a086c16d0a7c130855.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48807908' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48807908' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(233) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/V4bel/Januscape' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Januscape: Guest-to-Host Escape in KVM/x86 [CVE-2026-53359]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Januscape：KVM/x86 的来宾到宿主逃逸漏洞（CVE-2026-53359）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 10:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48807908' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48807908' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48807908'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Januscape discloses a guest-to-host escape vulnerability in KVM/x86 (CVE-2026-53359). The disclosure outlines potential exploitation paths and the urgency of applying patches across virtualization environments, from personal to cloud. It highlights ongoing hypervisor hardening challenges in modern virtualization stacks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Januscape 披露了 KVM/x86 的来宾到宿主逃逸漏洞（CVE-2026-53359）。公开信息描述了潜在的利用路径以及在虚拟化环境中应用补丁的紧迫性，无论是本地还是云端场景。凸显了现代虚拟化堆栈中超管道加固的持续挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/27f7fd2cc0e3c5be74594fdb9024c2335dc30237df12a713512940f64f53cb80/V4bel/Januscape' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/27f7fd2cc0e3c5be74594fdb9024c2335dc30237df12a713512940f64f53cb80/V4bel/Januscape' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48761868' data-tags='Career,Business,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48761868' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(234) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pluralistic.net/2026/07/02/canonization/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The difference between &quot;today&#x27;s task&quot; and &quot;accretive work&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>“今日任务”与“累积性工作”的区别</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 02, 2026 / 07:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48761868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48761868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48761868'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece contrasts finishing a single, isolated task with pursuing accretive work that compounds value over time. It argues that focusing only on short-term tasks can erode long-run impact unless you invest in infrastructure, documentation, and repeatable processes. For builders and researchers, the takeaway is to balance sprint work with durable foundations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中将单次、孤立任务与会带来复利效应的累积性工作进行对比。仅追求短期任务会削弱长期影响，需投入基础设施、文档和可重复的流程。对开发者和研究者而言，平衡短期冲刺与长期基础建设是关键。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/craphound.com/images/02Jul2026.jpg?w=840&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/craphound.com/images/02Jul2026.jpg?w=840&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48841348' data-tags='Hardware,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48841348' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(235) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.amazon.com/RISC-V-Microprocessor-System-Chip-Design/dp/0323994989' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Book: RISC-V System-on-Chip Design</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RISC-V 系统芯片设计（书籍）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 22:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48841348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48841348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48841348'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This book surveys how to design Systems-on-Chip around the RISC-V ISA, covering architecture, verification, toolchains, and ecosystem considerations. It helps engineers reason about open ISA advantages and practical design trade-offs for modern SoCs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本书系统介绍在 RISC-V 指令集上的系统芯片设计，包括架构、验证、工具链与生态等方面，帮助工程师理解开源 ISA 在现代硬件设计中的应用与取舍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://m.media-amazon.com/images/G/01/social_share/amazon_logo._CB635397845_.png' data-full='https://m.media-amazon.com/images/G/01/social_share/amazon_logo._CB635397845_.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48870799' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48870799' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(236) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tiki.li/blog/lucky_code.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Your code is fast if you&#x27;re lucky</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你的代码若走运才会快</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 03:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48870799' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48870799' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48870799'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Code speed often depends on luck as much as design—factors like compiler optimizations, CPU caches, and workload shape performance. The piece argues for data-oriented design, profiling, and robust benchmarks rather than chasing micro-optimizations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>代码的执行速度在很大程度上受外部因素影响，运气也很关键——编译器优化、CPU缓存和工作负载都会左右性能。文中倡导以数据为导向的设计、性能分析与可靠基准，避免盲目追逐微小优化。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48747889' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48747889' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(237) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rotman_lens' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rotman Lens</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rotman透镜</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 01, 2026 / 07:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48747889' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 128</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48747889' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48747889'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rotman lens is a compact microwave lens used for beamforming in RF and millimeter-wave systems. It enables multi-beam steering in a planar form, with applications in 5G, satellite, and radar tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rotman透镜是一种紧凑的微波透镜，用于射频/毫米波系统的波束成形。它在平面结构中实现多波束定向，广泛应用于5G、卫星通信和雷达领域。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/c/cd/Rotman_lens.jpg' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/c/cd/Rotman_lens.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48830868' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48830868' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(238) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theatlantic.com/magazine/2026/08/reading-crisis-postliterate-age/687618/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>It seems that the age of reading might be a short anomaly in human history</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阅读时代可能只是人类历史中的短暂异常</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 05:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48830868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 128</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48830868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 266</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48830868'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article argues that the era of widespread reading may be a temporary anomaly in a postliterate age, discussing cognitive, educational, and societal implications as digital media evolve. It challenges assumptions about literacy&#x27;s central role in the future.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章认为大众阅读的时代可能只是人类进入后读写时代的一个短暂异常，探讨数字媒介演进对认知、教育和社会的影响，挑战未来对识字地位的普遍假设。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.theatlantic.com/thumbor/TaSgM_kXEyiXXBzlvXfpi1XeN5c=/0x43:2000x1085/1200x625/media/img/2026/07/WEL_HorowitchOpenerHP-1/original.png' data-full='https://cdn.theatlantic.com/thumbor/TaSgM_kXEyiXXBzlvXfpi1XeN5c=/0x43:2000x1085/1200x625/media/img/2026/07/WEL_HorowitchOpenerHP-1/original.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48827058' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48827058' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(239) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://buf.build/blog/protobuf-py' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Protobuf-py: Protobuf for Python, without compromises</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Protobuf-py：Python 版 Protobuf，毫不妥协</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 20:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48827058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 127</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48827058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48827058'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Protobuf-py provides a Python-native Protobuf runtime with minimal compromises, ensuring seamless cross-language data interchange and performance. It enables efficient wire-format handling and smoother integration with services across languages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Protobuf-py 提供了一个 Python 原生的 Protobuf 运行时，强调兼容性和性能，避免妥协。它使跨语言的数据序列化/反序列化更加高效，便于与其他语言的服务对接。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://buf.build/images/buf-og-image-v1.png' data-full='https://buf.build/images/buf-og-image-v1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48786124' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48786124' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(240) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thisbugslife.com/2021/11/21/dolosse-a-south-african-invention-used-over-the-world/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dolosse – a South African invention used over the world</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Dolosse：一种南非发明现已全球应用的海岸结构</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 04, 2026 / 08:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 126</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48786124'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dolosse are interlocking concrete blocks that dissipate wave energy to protect coasts and harbors. Originating in South Africa, these modular structures have spread worldwide and are used on coastlines everywhere. Their enduring value lies in a low-cost, scalable approach to coastal defense.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dolosse 是互锁混凝土块，用于分散海岸和港口的波浪能量。起源于南非，如今全球广泛使用于海岸线，这种模块化结构以低成本、可扩展性著称，成为实用的海岸防护方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/thisbugslife.com/wp-content/uploads/2018/08/fullsizeoutput_1939.jpeg?fit=1200%2C900&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/thisbugslife.com/wp-content/uploads/2018/08/fullsizeoutput_1939.jpeg?fit=1200%2C900&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48829242' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(241) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/dekart-xyz/geosql' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Geosql: A Claude/Codex skill for geospatial data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Geosql：用于地理空间数据的 Claude/Codex 技能</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 01:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48829242' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48829242' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48829242'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Geosql provides a Claude/Codex skill for geospatial data, enabling LLMs to query maps, coordinates, and spatial relationships more effectively. This helps automate geo-enabled reasoning, map-based data extraction, and location-aware AI tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Geosql 提供了一套用于地理空间数据的 Claude/Codex 技能，使大语言模型能够更高效地查询地图、坐标和空间关系，提升地理推理与定位任务的自动化水平。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f9130fc5eda749d758327b4ec999b2eb87223f1ec6ad3429f6123c80c5859693/dekart-xyz/geosql' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f9130fc5eda749d758327b4ec999b2eb87223f1ec6ad3429f6123c80c5859693/dekart-xyz/geosql' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48826703' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48826703' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(242) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/interactive/finance-and-economics/2026/07/02/is-the-economist-always-wrong' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is The Economist Always Wrong?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>经济学家总是错的吗？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 19:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48826703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48826703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 124</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48826703'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An interactive examination of economist forecasting accuracy shows that macro predictions are inherently uncertain and frequently revised. It advocates probabilistic thinking and scenario planning over single-point forecasts, highlighting limits of models and data revisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过互动分析揭示经济学家的预测并非总能准确，宏观预测本质上存在不确定性且常被修订。文章提倡以概率思维和情景分析取代单点预测，强调模型局限与数据更新带来的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48879902' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(244) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ca.pcmag.com/social-media/16790/the-death-of-the-status-update-why-55-of-americans-stopped-posting-on-social-media' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Death of the Status Update: Why 55% of Americans Stopped Posting on Social Media</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>状态更新的时代结束：为何55%的美国人停止在社交媒体发帖</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 12, 2026 / 03:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48879902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48879902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48879902'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demographics, platform changes, and privacy fatigue help explain why 55% of Americans stopped posting on social media. The trend has implications for marketers, content creators, and platform design as attention shifts and new formats emerge. It underscores a pivot point in social communication patterns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人口结构变化、平台演变与隐私疲劳共同解释了为何约55%的美国人停止在社交媒体发帖。这一趋势将影响营销、内容创作者与平台设计，因为受众重心正在转移，新格式也在兴起，揭示社交沟通模式的转折点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sm.pcmag.com/t/pcmag_ca/news/t/the-death-/the-death-of-the-status-update-why-55-of-americans-stopped-p_esbq.1200.png' data-full='https://sm.pcmag.com/t/pcmag_ca/news/t/the-death-/the-death-of-the-status-update-why-55-of-americans-stopped-p_esbq.1200.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(245) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theconversation.com/the-u-s-just-approved-a-giant-space-mirror-to-test-sunlight-on-demand-low-earth-orbit-is-getting-weird-283482' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FCC approves test of space mirror to light night sky</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FCC批准测试太空镜以点亮夜空</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 05:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48871367' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48871367' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48871367'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The US FCC approves a test of a large space-based mirror to reflect sunlight for nighttime illumination. Potential benefits include energy-efficient lighting, but risks such as orbital debris, light pollution, and geopolitical concerns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国联邦通信委员会批准测试一个巨型太空镜，将太阳光投射到夜空以实现夜间照明。潜在优点包括节能照明，但也存在轨道碎片、光污染与地缘政治等风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.theconversation.com/files/747250/original/file-20260710-57-3s3smh.jpg?ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;rect=0%2C134%2C2400%2C1200&amp;q=45&amp;auto=format&amp;w=1356&amp;h=668&amp;fit=crop' data-full='https://images.theconversation.com/files/747250/original/file-20260710-57-3s3smh.jpg?ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;rect=0%2C134%2C2400%2C1200&amp;q=45&amp;auto=format&amp;w=1356&amp;h=668&amp;fit=crop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 05, 2026 / 06:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48794302' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48794302' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new es40 fork enables Windows 2000 to run on DEC Alpha hardware, a niche retro-computing achievement that demonstrates cross-architecture compatibility and enduring interest in old OS ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过对 es40 的分叉，在 DEC Alpha 硬件上实现 Windows 2000 的运行，展示了跨架构兼容性的潜力与对经典操作系统生态的持续热情。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48849744' data-tags='Science,Culture,AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48849744' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(247) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07267' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Billions of Sketches Reveal Hidden Cultural Variation in Human Concepts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数十亿幅素描揭示人类概念的隐藏文化差异</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 09, 2026 / 10:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48849744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48849744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48849744'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers analyze billions of sketches to show that concept representations vary across cultures, challenging universal assumptions in AI and cognitive science. The findings imply that AI systems trained on a single cultural dataset may misinterpret concepts in diverse user groups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者对数十亿幅素描进行分析，揭示人类概念在不同文化中存在显著差异，质疑AI与认知科学中的普遍性假设。结果意味着以单一文化数据集训练的AI系统在不同群体中的概念理解可能存在偏差。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48831587' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(248) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/2026/07/a-bug-which-only-affected-left-handed-users/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A bug which affected only left handed users</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>仅影响左撇子用户的漏洞</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 06:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48831587' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 119</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48831587' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48831587'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A quirky bug affected only left-handed users, revealing design gaps where user handedness wasn&#x27;t considered. The incident underscores the importance of inclusive testing and considering diverse interaction patterns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个仅影响左撇子用户的有趣漏洞暴露了设计阶段对使用习惯考虑不足。此事凸显了进行包容性测试和覆盖边缘用法的重要性，也提醒开发者在界面布局和交互设计时考虑不同用户群体。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48883238' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(249) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://substack.magazinenongrata.com/p/how-i-learned-to-read-again' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I Learned to Read Again</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我重新学会了阅读</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 12, 2026 / 11:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883238' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 119</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883238' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48883238'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal reflection on rediscovering reading as an adult, exploring shifts in focus, cognitive engagement, and the joy of absorbing written material.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者反思成年后重新拾起阅读的过程，谈及专注力、认知参与度的变化，以及重新获得阅读乐趣的体会。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(250) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/offbeat/2026/07/05/c-programmers-commit-fresh-crimes-against-readability/5265981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>C programmers commit fresh crimes against readability</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>C 程序员在可读性方面再犯错</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 04:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48803347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 118</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48803347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48803347'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique of C coding style that argues current practices harm readability, with examples of cryptic macros and opaque constructs. It offers guidance for writing clearer, maintainable C.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>批评当下 C 语言的编码风格，认为存在使可读性下降的做法，举出模糊的宏和不透明的代码结构，并给出更清晰、可维护的写法建议。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48847562' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(251) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.context.dev' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Context.dev (YC S26) – API to get structured data from any website</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Launch HN：Context.dev（YC S26）——从任意网站提取结构化数据的 API</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 09, 2026 / 08:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48847562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 116</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48847562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48847562'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Context.dev offers an API to extract structured data from any website, enabling developers to pull tables, specs, pricing, and metadata into applications. This can streamline data pipelines and improve automation for web scraping, SEO, and analytics, but raises questions about data licensing, accuracy, and anti-scraping protections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Context.dev 提供一个 API，可从任意网站提取结构化数据，如表格、规格、定价和元数据，便于接入到应用中。提高了数据管线的自动化和网页数据的可用性，但也带来数据授权、准确性和反爬策略等挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.context.dev/api/og?title=Every+piece+of+web+context+your+agent+needs.' data-full='https://www.context.dev/api/og?title=Every+piece+of+web+context+your+agent+needs.' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何选用 Vanilla JavaScript？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 12, 2026 / 15:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48885705' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48885705' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48885705'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues for sticking with plain JavaScript over frameworks, emphasizing simplicity, portability, and long-term maintainability. It warns that over-engineered toolchains often add overhead without proportional payoff.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张坚持使用纯 JavaScript 而非框架，强调简洁性、可移植性和长期维护性，以及避免被过度复杂的工具链拖累。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
