<p class='hn-title'>(254) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/07/10/technology/apple-openai-lawsuit.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple sues OpenAI, accusing it of stealing company secrets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果起诉 OpenAI，指控其窃取公司机密</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 14:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48865431' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48865431' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48865431'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple file a lawsuit against OpenAI alleging theft of company secrets, underscoring IP and confidential data concerns in AI development. The case could influence how enterprises manage data sharing and model training, and may shift competitive dynamics in the AI ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果就窃取公司机密向 OpenAI 提起诉讼，凸显在 AI 研发中对知识产权和机密数据的关注。此案可能影响企业在数据共享和模型训练方面的合规做法，并改变 AI 生态的竞争格局。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48870258' data-tags='Business,Health,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48870258' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(255) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/news/features/2026-07-07/why-it-s-so-difficult-to-produce-100-american-made-medical-gloves' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why it&#x27;s so difficult to produce American-made medical gloves</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么很难实现100%美国产医用手套</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 02:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48870258' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 112</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48870258' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48870258'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Delve into manufacturing constraints: raw materials (nitrile latex), specialized gloves, supply chain dependencies, domestic capacity, automation challenges, regulatory compliance, and cost pressures. Highlights that reshoring gloves is complex, with implications for healthcare resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>深入解释制造美国产医用手套的难点，包括原材料、产能、供应链依赖、以及合规成本。重塑本土产能面临技术与成本双重挑战，对医疗系统的韧性具有重要影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48881967'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google Research outlines how collaboration among cities, companies, and researchers can reduce traffic congestion through shared data, coordinated policies, and joint experiments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌研究指出，城市、企业与研究机构的协作通过数据共享、协同政策和联合试验，能够降低交通拥堵。文章强调通过数据共享、仿真等实际路径推动全系统的改进。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 04, 2026 / 10:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48787124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 111</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48787124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48787124'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Atlantic&#x27;s photo set chronicles the massive NASA wind tunnels, offering a visual history of how air testing enabled rocket and aircraft breakthroughs. The images reveal engineering scale and the hands-on era behind aerospace science.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《大西洋月刊》的一组照片记录了 NASA 巨大风洞的历史场景，直观呈现空气测试如何推动火箭与航空器的突破。照片揭示了工程规模和航空航天科学背后的动手实践时代。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(258) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.zksecurity.xyz/posts/circl-bugs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Meets Cryptography 1: What AI Found in Cloudflare&#x27;s Circl</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 与密码学 1：AI 在 Cloudflare Circl 中发现的漏洞与改进</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 11:36 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI-assisted auditing of Cloudflare Circl reveals findings about cryptographic primitives and implementations, illustrating how AI can aid security reviews. The piece underscores both benefits and the need for careful, human-verified validation in crypto code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过 AI 辅助对 Cloudflare Circl 的审计，揭示密码学原语与实现方面的发现，展示 AI 在安全评审中的潜在价值，同时也强调对密码库代码进行人工核验的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 15:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48811373' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48811373' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48811373'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenSSH 10.4 and 10.4p1 bring security fixes and performance improvements to the SSH ecosystem, with patch releases addressing additional issues. Upgrading is recommended for better security and reliability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenSSH 10.4 及 10.4p1 提供安全修复与性能改进，补丁版本修复了额外问题。建议升级以提升安全性与可靠性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48882554' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48882554' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(261) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Automation Without Understanding</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无需理解的自动化</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 12, 2026 / 09:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48882554'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Automation Without Understanding argues that autonomous systems can operate without deep human-like understanding, but this can yield brittleness and unsafe failures. The piece urges safeguards, monitoring, and clearly defined boundaries for automated agents. It underscores designing automation with verifiability and context in mind.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《无需理解的自动化》认为自动系统在缺乏深层人类式理解的情况下也能运行，但这会带来脆弱性与潜在的安全失效。文章呼吁加强安全措施、监控并为自动代理设定清晰边界，强调设计时应优先考虑可验证性与上下文。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48877214' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48877214' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(262) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://my.uscap.org/uscap/program/S0tc675/index.cfm?pgid=5167&amp;sid=14770&amp;abid=51228' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Autopsy Study Finds Replicating SARS-CoV-2 in the Hearts of Long Covid</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>尸检研究发现长年新冠患者心脏中存在 SARS‑CoV‑2</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 17:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48877214' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48877214' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48877214'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An autopsy study finds SARS-CoV-2 replication in the hearts of some long-COVID patients, shedding light on cardiac involvement and potential mechanisms behind lingering symptoms. The result underscores long-COVID&#x27;s potential organ-specific effects and the need for ongoing cardiovascular monitoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尸检研究发现部分长期新冠患者的心脏中存在 SARS‑CoV‑2 的复制，揭示心脏受累及长期症状的潜在机制。结果强调长期新冠对心血管健康的潜在影响，需持续监测。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://my.uscap.org/uscap/images/2026AnnualMeeting/header_1_v1.png?v=261207132703' data-full='https://my.uscap.org/uscap/images/2026AnnualMeeting/header_1_v1.png?v=261207132703' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48846332' data-tags='Politics,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48846332' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(263) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mitpress.mit.edu/9780262053198/simpolitics/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SimPolitics: America’s quest to solve politics with computers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SimPolitics：美国用计算机解决政治的探索</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 09, 2026 / 07:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48846332' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 107</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48846332' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48846332'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SimPolitics analyzes how computers are reshaping politics in the US, from data-driven governance to algorithmic decision-making and electoral tech. It discusses benefits and risks—transparency, bias, accountability—and argues technologists should consider civic implications in their work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SimPolitics 探讨计算机如何重塑美国政治，从数据驱动治理到算法决策和选举科技。讨论了透明度、偏见与问责等利与弊，并倡导科技从业者在工作中考虑公民社会的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48873855' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48873855' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(264) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pluralistic.net/2025/09/11/vulgar-thatcherism/#there-is-an-alternative' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reverse centaurs are the answer to the AI paradox (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>反向半人马：AI悖论的答案（2025）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 10:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48873855' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 107</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48873855' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48873855'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proposes &#x27;reverse centaurs&#x27;—humans guided by AI to amplify judgment and creativity—as a solution to AI paradoxes like misalignment and over-reliance on automation. Emphasizes human-AI collaboration as a productive path forward.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出“反向半人马”概念，即在人类决策中叠加AI辅助以增强判断力与创造力，作为应对AI悖论的路径。强调人机协作比单纯追求自动化更具生产力，适用于未来工作与创新。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/craphound.com/images/11Sep2025.jpg?w=840&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/craphound.com/images/11Sep2025.jpg?w=840&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48803791' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(265) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://manualmeida.dev/articles/noiselang/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NoiseLang: Where N = 5 is a Dirac delta</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NoiseLang：当 N = 5 时成为 Dirac Delta 的语言</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 05:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48803791' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48803791' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48803791'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NoiseLang presents a construction where a parameter choice (N=5) yields a Dirac delta, illustrating a playful connection between discrete parameterization and continuous functions in a toy language. It offers a curious glimpse into symbolic computation and language design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NoiseLang 展示了一个设定，在参数 N=5 时会得到 Dirac Delta，揭示离散参数与连续函数之间的有趣联系。这个小型语言为符号计算与语言设计提供了有趣的直观案例。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48819512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48819512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48819512'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Better Auth will join Vercel, integrating its authentication platform into Vercel&#x27;s hosting and deployment ecosystem. The integration simplifies secure sign-in flows for developers deploying on Vercel, potentially affecting pricing and vendor lock-in considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Better Auth 将与 Vercel 整合其身份验证平台，融入 Vercel 的托管与部署生态。该集成为在 Vercel 上部署的开发者简化了安全登录流程，同时可能影响定价与厂商锁定的考量。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into the audio issues common with budget HDMI-to-VGA adapters and practical hardware fixes. It shows how small DAC and wiring tweaks can restore analog audio without breaking the price point.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>针对低价 HDMI-to-VGA 适配器常见的模拟音频问题给出实用修复方法，展示通过微小的 DAC 与连线调整即可在不抬高成本的情况下恢复音频输出。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(269) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/large-language-models-ieee-course' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>IEEE Rolls Out Large Language Models Training Course</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>IEEE 推出大型语言模型培训课程</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 30, 2026 / 22:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48742703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48742703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>IEEE has launched a formal training course on Large Language Models, covering fundamentals of training, safety, evaluation, and governance. The program aims to standardize best practices and upskill practitioners amid rapid LLM developments. The course could influence enterprise adoption by providing structured, reputable education.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>IEEE 启动大型语言模型培训课程，覆盖训练、安全、评估和治理要点。该计划旨在统一行业实践，帮助从业者跟上快速发展的 LLM 领域。课程有望通过体系化、权威教育资源影响企业采用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(270) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://triapul.cz/automa/i_did_not_kill_stanley_lieber' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I Did Not Kill Stanley Lieber: How to Draw (With 9front)</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 09, 2026 / 07:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48846177' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48846177' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48846177'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A quirky exploration of drawing with 9front, a Plan 9-inspired OS, through a Stanley Lieber reference. The piece uses retro tooling to show how constraints can inspire creative programming experiments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以 9front 为背景的绘画实验，借用斯坦利·李的名字制造幽默参照，展示用 Plan 9 式系统进行图形创作的乐趣。通过受限工具链，体现了约束也能激发创作。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48829797' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48829797' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(271) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://seriot.ch/computation/uts35/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Unicode&#x27;s transliteration rules are Turing-complete</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Unicode 的转写规则具备图灵完备性</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 02:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48829797' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48829797' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48829797'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>UTS 35 transliteration rules can simulate a Turing machine, implying that text transformations under Unicode can express arbitrary computation. This raises questions about the limits of decidability and security in text-processing pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>UTS 35 的转写规则具备图灵完备性，理论上能把文本转换做成任意计算。这对文本处理管线的可判定性与安全性提出了新挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://seriot.ch/computation/uts35/uts35.png' data-full='https://seriot.ch/computation/uts35/uts35.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48780915' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48780915' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(272) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.uni-wuerzburg.de/en/news-and-events/news/detail/news/ameisen-kolonie-verletzte-pflegt/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ants: Who looks after the injured in a colony?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蚂蚁：蚁群里谁照看受伤者？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 03, 2026 / 15:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48780915' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48780915' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48780915'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers show ants nurse injured nestmates, revealing social care behaviors and a form of collective immunity in colonies. The findings highlight how simple local rules yield robust colony-level care and could inspire swarm robotics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究表明蚂蚁会照顾受伤的同巢成员，揭示群体内的社会照护与集体免疫现象。简单的局部规则能促成蚁群级别的可靠关爱，这对群体机器人也有启发。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.uni-wuerzburg.de/fileadmin/_processed_/e/e/csm_0702Ameisen-Pflegerinnen-www_d16a620736.jpg' data-full='https://www.uni-wuerzburg.de/fileadmin/_processed_/e/e/csm_0702Ameisen-Pflegerinnen-www_d16a620736.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48855043' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48855043' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(273) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://exitchatcontrol.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Exit Chat Control</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>退出聊天控制计划</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 09, 2026 / 19:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48855043' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48855043' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48855043'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Exit Chat Control describes a movement opposing mass surveillance policies that scan private messages. It frames digital rights and privacy as essential to free expression online, and discusses implications for platforms, policy, and users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>退出聊天控制是一场反对大规模监控私信的运动，强调数字权利与隐私对网络自由表达的重要性，并探讨对平台、政策和用户的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://exitchatcontrol.org/og.png' data-full='https://exitchatcontrol.org/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48877927' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48877927' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(274) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/ng-interactive/2026/jun/04/us-consumer-rage-prices-economy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why are US consumers so angry? It&#x27;s not just high prices</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国消费者为何如此愤怒？不仅是物价</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 20:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48877927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48877927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48877927'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deeper look beyond inflation at factors like stagnant wages, rising cost of living, perceived corporate excess, and political polarization contributing to consumer frustration. The piece suggests tech-enabled conveniences may mask economic stress and shape political sentiment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章在通胀之外探讨引发美国消费者愤怒的深入原因，包括工资停滞、生活成本上升、对企业过度利润的感知以及政治极化。指出科技带来的便利掩盖的经济压力，也在塑造公众情绪与政治态度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/caeb8d0189ea09e374076badf019fa85bc388f2a/45_52_3581_2866/master/3581.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=a8d3b633e4f46c8b71405859a1def195' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/caeb8d0189ea09e374076badf019fa85bc388f2a/45_52_3581_2866/master/3581.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=a8d3b633e4f46c8b71405859a1def195' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48814170' data-tags='AI,Hardware,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48814170' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(275) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mimo.xiaomi.com/blog/mimo-v2-5-inference' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Inference Optimization for MiMo v2.5: Pushing Hybrid SWA Efficiency to the Limit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MiMo v2.5 推理优化：把混合 SWA 的效率推向极限</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 23:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48814170' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 100</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48814170' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48814170'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MiMo v2.5 showcases inference optimization by combining SWA (Stochastic Weight Averaging) with hybrid strategies to squeeze efficiency for on-device AI. The update highlights gains in speed and energy efficiency for mobile models, enabling stronger performance within constrained hardware. This matters for mobile developers pushing AI features in edge devices and cameras.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MiMo v2.5 展示了将 SWA（随机权重平均）与混合策略结合的推理优化，在受限硬件上提升了速度与能耗效率。更新强调了移动模型在边缘设备上的性能提升，使在手机、相机等场景落地的 AI 功能更具竞争力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.cnbj1.fds.api.mi-img.com/aife/mimo-blog-fe/doc_build/static/image/logo.99baaffe.png' data-full='https://cdn.cnbj1.fds.api.mi-img.com/aife/mimo-blog-fe/doc_build/static/image/logo.99baaffe.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48882956' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48882956' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(276) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.motivenotes.ai/p/against-usefulness' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Against Usefulness</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>反对实用性</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 12, 2026 / 10:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882956' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882956' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48882956'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Against Usefulness argues that usefulness alone is an insufficient metric for evaluating technology; it advocates broader criteria like resilience, beauty, and social impact. The piece challenges builders to consider long-term trade-offs and unintended consequences. It proposes a more nuanced framework for value assessment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《反对实用性》认为仅以实用性作为评估技术的唯一标准不足以全面衡量价值，倡导将韧性、美感与社会影响等纳入评估。文章质疑开发者在设计时对长期取舍与潜在后果的忽视，并提出更为细致的价值评估框架。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!PChk!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F568f9825-5d39-466d-919c-45f7811bf475_1536x2048.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!PChk!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F568f9825-5d39-466d-919c-45f7811bf475_1536x2048.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48801493' data-tags='AI,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48801493' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(277) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tomtunguz.com/ai-spend-breakeven-2029/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When AI Costs More Than the Engineer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 成本何时会超过工程师成本</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 00:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48801493' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 97</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48801493' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 85</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48801493'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take on AI economics, arguing there will be a breakeven point where AI compute and data costs exceed human engineer salaries by 2029.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>从经济角度挑战 AI 投资的常见假设，预测到 2029 年 AI 的算力与数据成本可能超过工程师薪酬。讨论投资回报、成本控制，以及企业在 AI 方案中的预算分配。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://res.cloudinary.com/dzawgnnlr/image/upload/q_auto/f_auto/w_auto/v1782758517/odtjqwtlphmuix5rxxi1.png' data-full='https://res.cloudinary.com/dzawgnnlr/image/upload/q_auto/f_auto/w_auto/v1782758517/odtjqwtlphmuix5rxxi1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48877668' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48877668' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(278) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/we-know-simple-fluids-can-flow-turns-out-some-can-fracture-20260710/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Unexpected Solidlike Fracture in Simple Liquids</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>简单液体的意外固态断裂</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 19:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48877668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 97</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48877668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48877668'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Some simple liquids can fracture in a solid-like manner under certain conditions, challenging the conventional view that fluids always flow. The finding informs material science, phase transitions, and potential novel fluids with tunable fracture behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一些简单液体在特定条件下会呈现与流体不同的固态断裂行为，挑战了“流动就会流”的直觉。此发现有助于理解相变、材料科学，以及具可控断裂特性的新型流体。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/CrackingLiquids-cr-Adapted-with-permission-from-Phys.-Rev.-Lett.-136-124002.-Copyrighted-by-the-American-Physical-Society.-Social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/CrackingLiquids-cr-Adapted-with-permission-from-Phys.-Rev.-Lett.-136-124002.-Copyrighted-by-the-American-Physical-Society.-Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48798247' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48798247' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(279) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nicole.express/2026/phase-altering-by-line.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Composite Video on the NES: Why&#x27;s it so wobbly?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NES 的复合视频：为什么会抖动？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 05, 2026 / 14:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48798247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48798247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48798247'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The wobble of NES composite video comes from analog signal limits, color encoding quirks, and timing jitter in older hardware. The piece explains why this classic setup is inherently unstable and what modern restorers can do to stabilize or emulate the signal.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NES 复合视频抖动的根源在于模拟信号的局限、颜色编码和时序抖动等因素。文中解释了为何这套经典设置易产生不稳定，并给出硬件改造或仿真再现的思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nicole.express/assets/img/wobbly/frames.gif' data-full='https://nicole.express/assets/img/wobbly/frames.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48825216' data-tags='Science,Health,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48825216' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(280) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alephneuro.com/blog/silent-speech' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Silent speech with ultrasound</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>超声实现的无声语言</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 16:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48825216' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48825216' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48825216'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers use ultrasound to capture articulatory movements or to stimulate vocal tract, enabling silent speech communication. The approach could empower people with speech impairments and enable quieter communication in noisy environments, but faces challenges in accuracy, privacy, and real-time practicality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究团队利用超声波捕捉舌、口腔等发音器官运动，或用于刺激声道，从而实现无声语音通信。这项技术对语言障碍者和嘈杂环境中的安静交流具有吸引力，但在准确性、隐私与实时性方面仍面临挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://alephneuro.com/blog/silent-speech/probe-setup.png' data-full='https://alephneuro.com/blog/silent-speech/probe-setup.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48839397' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48839397' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(281) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.liamcvw.com/p/remote-attestation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Remote Attestation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>远程证明</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 17:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48839397' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48839397' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48839397'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Remote attestation cryptographically proves a system’s software and hardware state to a remote party, a cornerstone of trusted computing and supply-chain security. It also introduces privacy and scalability considerations in large distributed setups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>远程证明让设备能向远端对等方以密码学方式证明自身的软件与硬件状态，是可信计算与供应链安全的核心。但在大规模分布式系统中也带来隐私与扩展性的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!A0EF!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F6ab9008a-68c9-4f18-8615-2d1d4462dbcd_1536x1024.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!A0EF!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F6ab9008a-68c9-4f18-8615-2d1d4462dbcd_1536x1024.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>机制性可解释性研究者把因果理论应用于大语言模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 12, 2026 / 11:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48883090'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mechanistic interpretability researchers are applying causality theory to LLMs to uncover causal structures behind reasoning and behavior. This shift aims to move beyond correlations toward interpretable cause-effect dynamics, aiding debugging and safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>机制性可解释性研究者正在把因果理论引入大语言模型，试图揭示模型推理和行为背后的因果结构。目标是从单纯相关性转向可因果推理的解释框架，帮助调试和提升安全性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(305) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/karola3vax/CS2FOW' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CS2 Fog Of War: Server-sided anti-wallhack occlusion culling for CS2 servers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CS2 Fog Of War：服务器端的遮挡剔除以对抗穿墙作弊</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 08:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48805965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48805965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48805965'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CS2 Fog Of War implements server-side occlusion culling to hide unseen geometry, reducing information leakage and helping prevent wallhack-style cheating. The approach shifts some visibility logic to the server, potentially impacting latency and fairness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CS2 Fog Of War 在服务器端实现遮挡剔除，隐藏玩家看不到的几何信息，降低穿墙作弊的可能性。该做法将可见性的一部分放在服务器端，可能对延迟与游戏公平性产生影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1289734986/67850766-4b8c-42ec-a0ba-1ca35ee824f5' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1289734986/67850766-4b8c-42ec-a0ba-1ca35ee824f5' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(306) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chronicle.software/insights/blogs/why-low-latency-java-still-requires-discipline' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why low-latency Java still requires discipline?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么低延迟 Java 仍然需要自律？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 06:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48804017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48804017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48804017'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Even with modern JVMs, achieving low latency demands disciplined practices around garbage collection, thread scheduling, and I/O management. The piece shares practical strategies for keeping jitter and pauses under control in latency-sensitive apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>即便在现代 JVM 的帮助下，实现低延迟仍需在垃圾回收、线程调度和输入输出管理等方面保持自律。文章分享在对延迟敏感的应用中控制抖动与暂停的实用策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(309) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://postgresisenough.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Do you need separate systems when you already have Postgres?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>当你已经有 Postgres 时，还需要分离的系统吗？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 07:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48805564' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48805564' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48805564'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that for many workloads, Postgres can handle multiple tasks through proper schema design, extensions, and tuning, questioning the default urge to split systems. Rethinking data architecture can reduce complexity and operational cost.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为在许多场景下，Postgres 通过合适的模式设计、扩展和调优即可应对多种负载，质疑将系统拆分开的常规做法。重新思考数据架构可以降低复杂性与运维成本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://postgresisenough.com/og-image.png' data-full='https://postgresisenough.com/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 05:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48816900' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48816900' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48816900'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A thorough benchmark compares PostgreSQL performance and cost across 23 EC2 instance types, highlighting how memory, CPU, and I/O patterns affect dollars per transaction. Key takeaways help operators pick a cost-effective deployment strategy on AWS.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇全面基准测试在 23 种 EC2 实例类型上比较 PostgreSQL 的性能与成本，揭示内存、CPU 和 I/O 模式如何影响每笔交易的花费。研究要点帮助运维者在 AWS 上选择具成本效益的部署策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48880406' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(316) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mrbruh.com/motorola/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Unauthenticated RCE in Motorola&#x27;s MR2600 Router</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>摩托罗拉 MR2600 路由器存在未授权远程代码执行漏洞</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 12, 2026 / 04:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48880406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48880406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48880406'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers found an unauthenticated remote code execution flaw in Motorola MR2600 routers, enabling attackers to take control without credentials. The issue spotlights consumer routers as persistent attack surfaces and the importance of firmware updates. Users should patch promptly and consider upgrading devices with better security guarantees.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现摩托罗拉 MR2600 路由器存在未授权远程代码执行漏洞，攻击者无需凭证即可控制设备。这暴露了消费级路由器作为持续的攻击面，以及固件更新的重要性。用户应尽快打补丁，并考虑升级到更具安全保障的设备。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mrbruh.com/motorola/motorola_advert.avif' data-full='https://mrbruh.com/motorola/motorola_advert.avif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

They provide a blueprint for building robust download tooling in networked environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Yt-dlp 的序列图揭示视频下载管线的精确流程，从元数据获取到区段选择与重试。图示呈现边界情况、错误处理与潜在优化点，便于贡献者和用户改进工具。为在网络环境中构建稳健下载工具提供蓝图。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48807108' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(318) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48807108' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: Are systems ready for the first negative leap second?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN：系统是否已为首个负闰秒做好准备？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 09:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48807108' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48807108' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48807108'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The question asks whether existing systems can handle a negative leap second, a hypothetical situation that would subtract time from a day. It highlights concerns about timekeeping, clock synchronization, and time APIs, inviting engineers to share preparedness, mitigations, and past experiences. A reminder of the fragility of digital timekeeping.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这个提问探讨若出现首个负闰秒，现有系统是否能够承受在一天中“减去时间”的情景，涉及时钟同步、时间 API 与时间管理的鲁棒性。邀请工程师分享应对策略、缓解措施和相关经验，提醒数字时间管理的脆弱性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 改变软件重写的经济学</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 22:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48841446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48841446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 85</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48841446'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI lowers the cost of rewriting software, accelerating migrations and refactors, and shifting how teams plan tech debt work. The article argues the &#x27;slop&#x27; in codebase begins with the base code, suggesting organizations should rethink architecture and test strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 减少软件重写成本，可能加速迁移与重构，并改变团队处理技术债务的方式。文中指出代码基线质量是关键，建议重新审视架构与测试策略。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 09, 2026 / 04:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48844046' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48844046' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wyrm lets users solve algebraic problems through touch-based interaction, built on an open-source soundness engine. The project demonstrates novel human-computer interaction for math education and accessibility, potentially enabling new ways to teach and explore algebraic concepts. It also provides a glimpse into how formal-method tooling can empower creative coding.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48877178' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(324) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://abcnews.com/US/wireStory/martha-lillard-us-polio-patient-iron-lung-dies-134668491' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Martha Lillard, last US polio patient using iron lung, dies at 78 in Oklahoma</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国最后一位使用铁肺的脊髓灰质炎患者在俄克拉荷马去世</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 17:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48877178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48877178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48877178'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Martha Lillard, the last U.S. polio patient using an iron lung, dies at 78 in Oklahoma, marking the end of an era in medical history. The obituary reflects on polio, medical technology, and societal changes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国最后一位使用铁肺的脊髓灰质炎患者玛莎·利拉德在俄克拉荷马州去世，享年78岁，标志着一个医疗历史时代的结束。对脊髓灰质炎、医疗技术及社会变迁的反思。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48831886' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48831886' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48831886'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Windows desktop OS share falls below 60% globally for the first time in years, as macOS, Linux, and alternative platforms gain traction. The shift could influence enterprise IT, software ecosystems, and vendor competition.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Windows 桌面操作系统全球市场份额首次跌破 60%，MacOS、Linux 等平台开始获得市场份额回升。此趋势可能影响企业 IT、软件生态和厂商竞争格局。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The AI buildout is slowed by grid and energy constraints, requiring improvements in hardware efficiency and data-center infrastructure. The article argues that power and cooling, not just compute, bottleneck AI deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 推进受限于电网和能源约束，需要提升硬件效率与数据中心基础设施。文章强调功率与冷却等因素，而非仅仅是计算能力，是 AI 部署的瓶颈。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WhimFiles 提供一个快速的原生 Mac 文件管理器，只有 9 MB，支持筛选与模糊查找且不依赖 Electron。该轻量化实现吸引寻求速度与低内存占用的开发者。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(333) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://questdb.com/blog/jvm-jit-known-bits/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The mask that compiles to nothing: how HotSpots JIT learned to reason about bits</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>可编译成零的掩码：HotSpot JIT 如何对比特位进行推理</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 05, 2026 / 15:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48798566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 73</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48798566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48798566'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HotSpot JIT can use bit-level reasoning to optimize away masking work, turning what looks like meaningful code into lean machine instructions. This reveals how compiler optimizations quietly boost performance for heavy JVM workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HotSpot JIT 如何通过位级推理把看似有意义的掩码运算优化成零成本甚至被省略的指令。揭示编译器优化在高性能 JVM 工作负载中的作用与影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48757900' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(337) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://geastack.com/blog-the-age-of-personalized-hardware-is-coming' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Age of Personalized Hardware Is Coming</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>个性化硬件时代正在到来</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 02, 2026 / 00:42 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48757900' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48757900' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48757900'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Personalized hardware is expanding from consumer devices to modular systems and bespoke silicon choices, driven by AI workloads and longer device lifecycles. The trend challenges mass-market homogenization and could unlock efficient, user-specific performance, while raising questions about supply chains, privacy, and upgrade paths.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>个性化硬件时代正在到来，未来将从通用设备走向模块化、定制化的硬件设计。此趋势可能带来更高性价比与长期可升级性，但也引发供应链、隐私与升级路径等新问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://geastack.com/og-cover.jpg' data-full='https://geastack.com/og-cover.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(340) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/514-labs/dnsglobe' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DNSGlobe – Rust TUI to watch DNS propagate around the world</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 05, 2026 / 14:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48798313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48798313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DNSGlobe is a Rust-based TUI that visualizes DNS propagation around the world, offering a lightweight tool for operators to observe latency, regional differences, and caching behavior. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DNSGlobe 是基于 Rust 的 TUI 工具，用以可视化全球范围内的 DNS 传播情况，方便运维观察延迟和区域差异。展示了在运维场景中，文本界面也能提供实用的网络洞察。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 04, 2026 / 05:57 PT</span></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48785102'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EF Core 11 accelerates split queries, reducing the overhead of separate database trips for related data. Large-scale apps with complex includes can see lower latency and improved throughput.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>EF Core 11 对拆分查询进行了优化，降低了对相关数据分多次查询的开销。处理大型、复杂包含的应用时，可能实现更低延迟和更高吞吐。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(345) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hidden-phenomena.com/articles/modular' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why study Diophantine equations?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何研究丢番图方程？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 12, 2026 / 08:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48882097'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Diophantine equations illustrate deep links between number theory and computation, with implications for cryptography and algorithm design. Studying them sharpens problem-solving instincts and provides transferable mathematical tools, aided by modular techniques.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>丢番图方程揭示数论与计算之间的深层联系，对密码学与算法设计具有重要影响。研究它们能磨炼解决问题的直觉，并提供可迁移的数学工具，尤其在模方法等技术方面。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48779844' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(346) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.notebookcheck.net/Camera-with-transparent-display-launches-for-the-equivalent-of-29.1334495.0.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Camera with transparent display launches for the equivalent of $29</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>透明显示屏相机以29美元上市</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 03, 2026 / 13:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48779844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48779844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48779844'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A camera with a transparent display hits the market for about $29, enabling see-through visuals and AR-style overlays at a rock-bottom price. The device could unlock new hobbyist projects and drive interest in transparent-display technologies, though durability and image quality will be key questions. This price point could pressure bigger players to offer cheaper, semi-transparent options.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>透明显示屏摄像头以约29美元上市，使透视画面与叠加信息的增强现实场景成为可能。该设备可能催生新的爱好者项目并推动透明显示技术的普及，但耐用性与画质仍是关键问题；低价可能给大型厂商带来更便宜、半透明选项的压力。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48748522' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48748522' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(347) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://asteriskmag.com/issues/14/in-praise-of-observational-evidence' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>In Praise of Observational Evidence</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>论观测证据的价值</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 01, 2026 / 08:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48748522' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 66</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48748522' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48748522'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Observational evidence is celebrated as grounding knowledge in real-world data, complementing theory and controlled experiments. The piece argues that careful observation can reveal patterns experiments miss, while acknowledging limitations and the need for rigorous interpretation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>观测证据被视为将理论与现实数据联系起来的重要基石，补充了受控实验的不足。文章认为细致的观察能揭示实验未能发现的模式，同时也承认其局限性与需要严格解读的前提。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://asteriskmag.com/media/pages/issues/14/in-praise-of-observational-evidence/a697a19981-1782768020/rcts-lennart-finke-1600x900-crop-sharpen50.png' data-full='https://asteriskmag.com/media/pages/issues/14/in-praise-of-observational-evidence/a697a19981-1782768020/rcts-lennart-finke-1600x900-crop-sharpen50.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(352) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/jul/11/microsoft-amazon-google-datacentre-carbon-emissions-france' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Datacentres drive up big tech&#x27;s carbon emissions to a third of those of France</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数据中心让大型科技公司的碳排放达到法国的三分之一</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 12, 2026 / 03:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48879950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48879950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48879950'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Datacenters push big tech’s carbon footprint to roughly one third of France’s total emissions. The data-center energy profile, cooling needs, and efficiency improvements shape policy, pricing, and business strategies for cloud providers. This underscores the climate impact of on-demand computing at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>数据中心将大型科技公司的碳排放推升至大约等于法国总排放量的三分之一。数据中心的能源结构、冷却需求及效率提升将影响云服务的政策、定价与商业策略，凸显大规模按需计算的气候影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(354) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/news/ben-bernanke' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ben Bernanke Joins Anthropic Oversight Trust</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 09, 2026 / 19:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48855112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48855112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48855112'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Former Fed chair Ben Bernanke joins Anthropic&#x27;s Oversight Trust to help guide responsible AI development. <div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48841385' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(355) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dfarq.homeip.net/how-donkey-kong-toppled-atari/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Donkey Kong Toppled Atari</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《Donkey Kong》如何让 Atari 崩盘</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 22:34 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48841385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48841385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48841385'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Donkey Kong&#x27;s arcade success helped topple Atari by exposing its hardware-focused limits and strategy missteps in the early 1980s. The piece traces licensing dynamics, design choices, and market shifts that reshaped the home gaming landscape.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《Donkey Kong》在街机市场的成功暴露了雅达利以硬件为核心的商业模式的脆弱性，促成了80年代的行业动荡。文章回顾授权、设计选择与市场演变，解释一款游戏如何改变了整个游戏产业的格局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/dfarq.homeip.net/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/donkey-kong-social.jpg?fit=728%2C408&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/dfarq.homeip.net/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/donkey-kong-social.jpg?fit=728%2C408&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48804801' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48804801' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(356) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.npmjs.com/package/@atharva-again/pi-tg' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I Built a Telegram Client for Pi</a></p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48816724' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(358) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Rodiun/frugon' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Frugon – Find which LLM calls a cheaper model could handle (local, MIT)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Frugon—找出哪些 LLM 调用可以由更便宜的本地模型处理（MIT 开源）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 05:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48816724' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48816724' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48816724'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Frugon is a local-first tool that analyzes prompts to determine whether a cheaper model could handle the LLM call, helping teams cut inference costs without sacrificing quality. Built with MIT-licensed tooling, it helps teams optimize inference spend in real-world workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Frugon 是一款本地优先的工具，能够分析提示并判断是否可由更便宜的模型处理某些 LLM 调用，从而降低推理成本。该工具使用本地托管、MIT 许可的组件，帮助团队在实际工作负载中优化成本。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48797443' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(365) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://medium.com/@devli0/b-tree-indexes-in-postgresql-part-1-theory-eb2668c52520' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Understanding B-Tree Indexes in PostgreSQL: A Comprehensive Guide– Part 1</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PostgreSQL 中的 B-Tree 索引详解（第一部分）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 05, 2026 / 12:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48797443' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48797443' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48797443'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Part 1 introduces B-tree index theory, structure, and how PostgreSQL uses them to speed lookups. It discusses maintenance costs, vacuum implications, and when to choose B-trees over other index types.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>第一部分介绍 B-tree 索引的理论基础、结构以及 PostgreSQL 如何利用它们来加速查询。还讨论了维护成本、清理对性能的影响，以及在 PostgreSQL 中何时选择 B-tree 而非其他索引。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(366) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cam.ac.uk/research/news/first-use-of-precision-editing-to-study-human-embryo-development-reveals-role-of-master-gene' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using precision editing to study human embryo development shows master gene</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用精准编辑研究人类胚胎发育，揭示主控基因</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 02, 2026 / 19:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48769854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48769854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48769854'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers used precise genetic editing to study early human embryo development and identified a master gene that orchestrates key developmental steps. The work advances understanding of human development while raising biosafety and ethical considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员通过精准编辑研究人类胚胎发育，发现一个主控基因在关键发育步骤中发挥重要作用。该研究提升了对人类早期发育的理解，同时也带来生物安全与伦理方面的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48884992' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(367) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kode.diy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kode Dot Programmable pocket device for makers, pentesters and geeks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kode Dot：面向制造者、渗透测试人员与极客的可编程口袋设备</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 12, 2026 / 14:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48884992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48884992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(373) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nekrozqliphort.github.io/posts/membarrier/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>C++ Details of Asymmetric Fences</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>C++ 非对称屏障的细节分析</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 03, 2026 / 10:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48777488' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48777488' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48777488'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post explains asymmetric fences in C++, illustrating how non-symmetric memory barriers can improve performance while preserving correctness with careful ordering guarantees. It covers design tips and potential pitfalls when modern CPUs reorder memory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文解读 C++ 中的非对称屏障，说明在严格的排序保证下，非对称内存屏障如何提升性能并保持正确性。还讨论了在现代 CPU 可能的内存重排序场景中的设计要点与潜在坑。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48859298' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48859298' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Europe&#x27;s largest unions push for the right to cancel work on days exceeding 30°C, citing worker safety and climate resilience. The stance signals growing labor activism around extreme heat and could influence workplace policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲最大的工会要求在气温超过 30°C 时有权取消工作，以保障工人安全和应对极端高温。此举体现日益增长的劳动行动主义，或影响企业的工作制度与安全政策。</p>
