<p class='hn-title'>(377) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dynomight.net/hazard-ratios/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Life with Hazard Ratios</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>带着风险比的生活</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 08:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48806305' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 58</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48806305' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48806305'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An accessible primer on hazard ratios, explaining how they measure relative instantaneous risk in survival analysis and how to interpret them correctly in medical studies. It contrasts hazard ratios with risk ratios, and discusses common pitfalls like non-proportional hazards, offering practical intuition for researchers and readers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文对风险比（hazard ratio）进行了通俗讲解，阐明其在生存分析中衡量相对即时风险的含义以及如何在研究中正确解读。作者对比了风险比与风险相对比的区别，并讨论了如比例风险假设不成立等常见误区，为研究者提供直观的解读要点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dynomight.net/img/hazard-ratios/shooting-the-rapids.jpg' data-full='https://dynomight.net/img/hazard-ratios/shooting-the-rapids.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48809916' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(378) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://atthis.link/blog/2026/57207.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ditching Zotero for a Text File</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>放弃 Zotero，改用文本文件管理参考文献</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 13:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48809916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48809916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48809916'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An academic writer documents ditching Zotero in favor of plain text references to regain portability, privacy, and long-term access. The approach favors lightweight tools, markdown workflows, and BibTeX over centralized databases. It highlights a growing trend toward plaintext-first bibliography management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位学者记录放弃 Zotero，改用纯文本引用以提升可移植性、隐私和长期访问性。此做法倾向使用轻量工具、Markdown 工作流与 BibTeX，而非集中数据库。反映出向文本优先的参考文献管理的日益趋势。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何我们要放弃 Cloudflare Durable Objects</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 09, 2026 / 07:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48846757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48846757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48846757'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The team explains why they moved away from Cloudflare Durable Objects, citing latency, consistency, cost, and architectural complexity; discusses alternatives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>团队解释了放弃 Cloudflare Durable Objects 的原因，涉及延迟、数据一致性、成本与架构复杂性，并讨论了替代方案。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48800930' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48800930' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(389) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/aatishb/patterncollider' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Patterncollider: Generate and explore quasiperiodic tiling patterns</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Patterncollider：生成与探索准周期镶嵌图案</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 05, 2026 / 22:26 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48800930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48800930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48800930'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces Patterncollider, a tool to generate and explore quasiperiodic tilings, enabling interactive study of non-repeating patterns like Penrose tilings. It invites mathematical exploration and potential applications in art and game design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 Patterncollider，这一工具用于生成与探索准周期镶嵌图案，支持对非重复图案（如 Penrose 镶嵌）进行交互式研究。适用于数学爱好者，以及在艺术和游戏设计中的潜在应用。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48815117' data-tags='AI,Startups,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48815117' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(393) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.fastcompany.com/91520702/y-combinator-garry-tan-agentic-ai-social-media' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>YC CEO says he ships 37K LoC AI code per day. A developer looked under the hood</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>YC CEO称每日交付3.7万行AI代码；开发者探究内幕</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 01:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48815117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48815117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48815117'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Garry Tan claims YC teams ship 37k lines of AI-focused code per day, signaling rapid iteration in the startup accelerator ecosystem. A developer who looked under the hood found most work to be scaffolding and integration rather than novel models, illustrating how speed comes from tooling and workflow, not just fresh algorithms. The takeaway: velocity in AI tooling comes from composability and workflow automation more than new algorithms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Garrry Tan 表示YC团队每日交付约3.7万行AI相关代码，体现创业加速器生态的快速迭代。深入调查的人发现其中多为脚手架、工具与整合代码，而非全新的模型，说明速度更多来自工具链和工作流的高效组合，而非新算法本身。核心结论是，AI开发的高效来自可组合的工作流与自动化，而非凭空产生的新模型。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48862086' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48862086' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(398) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Cpp2Rust/cpp2rust' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cpp2Rust: Translates C++ to safe Rust automatically</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cpp2Rust：自动将 C++ 转换为安全 Rust</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 09:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48862086' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48862086' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48862086'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cpp2Rust automatically translates C++ code to Rust with safety guarantees, easing migration of legacy codebases. While it promises safer memory management, developers should scrutinize correctness and performance post-migration and plan for integration challenges. It opens a path to safer reimplementations of old code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cpp2Rust 能自动将 C++ 代码转换为具有安全性保证的 Rust，帮助遗留代码的迁移。虽然承诺更安全的内存管理，但迁移后仍需对正确性和性能进行审查，并考虑集成中的挑战。为旧代码提供了更安全的重实现路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/d9dfea1fc5acacb3711309f37b7d648aa88ab21d70fdb97eed52f52cfd7de498/Cpp2Rust/cpp2rust' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/d9dfea1fc5acacb3711309f37b7d648aa88ab21d70fdb97eed52f52cfd7de498/Cpp2Rust/cpp2rust' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48878195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48878195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48878195'>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为在 2026 年写代码依然重要，但趋势指向使用 AI 助手、提高抽象层级的工具以及可持续的软件实践。应选择真正需要编码的问题，并围绕可维护性来设计系统。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(401) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/society/2026/jul/07/mens-average-testosterone-levels-have-halved-in-last-50-years-say-scientists' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Men&#x27;s average testosterone levels have halved in last 50 years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>过去50年男性平均睾酮水平下降约一半</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 13:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48823368' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48823368' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48823368'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers report that men&#x27;s average testosterone has halved over the last 50 years, a trend tied to environmental factors, lifestyle changes, and health concerns. The finding could influence public health research and discussions around lifestyle, fertility, and aging. The article adds to a broader conversation about hormonal health trends.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示，过去50年男性平均睾酮水平下降约一半，这一趋势可能与环境因素、生活方式及健康问题相关。此发现将影响公共卫生研究和关于生活方式、生育与衰老的讨论，成为激素健康趋势话题的又一个证据。</p>

The approach reduces errors and makes Excel-driven processes scalable for analysts and admins.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示如何用 Python 将重复的 Excel 任务变成一键工作流，覆盖数据清洗、转换和报表生成的自动化，提升速度与可复用性。此方法可降低错误，使基于 Excel 的流程对分析师和管理员更具扩展性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(412) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lists.osgeo.org/pipermail/qgis-developer/2026-July/068430.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anyone else get a vague GitHub shakedown notice?</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 09:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48862467' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48862467' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48862467'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>People report receiving vague enforcement or policy notices from GitHub about project requirements, creating uncertainty for maintainers and contributors. The thread underscores tensions between platform governance and robust open-source workflows, and the need for clearer guidance. It’s a heads-up for teams relying on GitHub for collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有网友反映收到来自 GitHub 的模糊执法/政策通知，关于项目要求，给维护者与贡献者带来不确定性。这一讨论凸显平台治理与开源工作流之间的紧张关系，以及对更清晰指引的需求。提醒依赖 GitHub 进行协作的团队注意。</p>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48804016' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(416) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stalw.art/blog/dkim2-dmarcbis/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DKIM2 and DMARCbis Have Landed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DKIM2 与 DMARCbis 已落地</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 06:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48804016' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48804016' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48804016'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DKIM2 and DMARCbis bring modernized email authentication with improved signing and alignment, reducing spoofing risk and simplifying deployment. The new specs aim to streamline interoperability across mail systems while tightening security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DKIM2 与 DMARCbis 引入更现代的电子邮件认证机制，提升签名和对齐效果，降低伪造风险并简化部署。新标准还加强跨系统的互操作性与安全性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://stalw.art/og/blog/dkim2-dmarcbis.png' data-full='https://stalw.art/og/blog/dkim2-dmarcbis.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48823803' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(417) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://satteri.bruits.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Satteri: A Markdown pipeline forged in Rust for the JavaScript world</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Satteri：用 Rust 为 JavaScript 世界打造的 Markdown 流水线</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 14:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48823803' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48823803' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48823803'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Satteri is a Rust-powered Markdown pipeline designed for the JavaScript world, offering fast, safe parsing and transformation for JS tooling. By bridging Rust performance with JS ecosystems, it aims to speed up builds and improve reliability of content pipelines. It highlights a broader trend of polyglot tooling in developer workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Satteri 是一个用 Rust 构建的 Markdown 流水线，面向 JavaScript 生态，提供快速且安全的解析与转换，提升 JS 工具链的性能与可靠性。通过把 Rust 的性能与 JS 生态融合，旨在加速构建流程并提升内容管线的稳定性，体现了开发工作流中日益增多的多语言工具趋势。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 22:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48813850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48813850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48813850'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Odin 1.0 marks a milestone for a compact, pragmatic systems language focused on simplicity and performance. The release signals growing maturity, with core tooling and ecosystem ambitions appealing to developers seeking a lighter alternative to C and C++.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Odin 1.0 标志着这一紧凑、务实的系统语言在简洁性和性能方面迈出重要一步。发布带来核心工具链与生态建设的野心，可能吸引寻求比C/C++更轻量替代方案的开发者。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48831814' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48831814' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(419) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pvk.ca/Blog/2019/01/09/preemption-is-gc-for-memory-reordering/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Preemption is GC for memory reordering (2019)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>抢占即内存重排序的垃圾回收</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 06:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48831814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48831814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48831814'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Preemption is GC for memory ordering by interrupting execution and breaking pathological reorderings. By forcing context switches and isolating critical sections, preemption reduces the need for heavy memory barriers and simplifies reasoning about concurrency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>抢占被视为内存重排序的一种垃圾回收机制，通过中断执行来打断可能引起的异常重排序。通过强制切换和隔离临界区，抢占可以降低对昂贵内存屏障的需求，同时简化对并发的推理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pvk.ca/images/2019-01-09-preemption-is-gc-for-memory-reordering/unsafe-waitqueue-ssww.png' data-full='https://pvk.ca/images/2019-01-09-preemption-is-gc-for-memory-reordering/unsafe-waitqueue-ssww.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>X402 explores static-site monetization, testing approaches like sponsorship, paid content, or lightweight ads to monetize blogs without dynamic runtimes.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(422) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://marc.info/?l=openbsd-misc&amp;m=119318909016582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Theo de Raadt: &quot;You&#x27;ve been smoking something mind altering&quot; (2007)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Theo de Raadt：‘你一直在吸食某种会改变脑子的东西’（2007）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 12, 2026 / 09:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882189' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882189' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48882189'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2007 remark from Theo de Raadt, OpenBSD founder, illustrating his blunt stance on security, quality, and candor in open‑source culture. The quote captures the no‑nonsense ethos that shaped early BSD developments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>来自 OpenBSD 创始人 Theo de Raadt 的 2007 年言论，展现了他在安全、质量与开源文化中的直率风格。此类直言不讳的态度塑造了早期 BSD 的发展风格。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48882034' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48882034' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(423) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/schollz/croc/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Croc: Securely transfer files and folders between two computers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Croc：在两台计算机之间安全传输文件和文件夹</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 12, 2026 / 08:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882034' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882034' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48882034'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Croc is a simple CLI tool that securely transfers files between machines via encrypted channels, emphasizing ease of use and cross‑platform compatibility.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(424) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/robertsdotpm/runloom' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Runloom – Go-style coroutines for Python free-threaded</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 06:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48859387' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48859387' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48859387'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Runloom brings Go-style coroutines to Python, enabling more approachable concurrency without heavy threading. It could ease async programming for Python developers and spark new patterns for parallel tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Runloom 将 Go 风格的协程带进 Python，使并发编程更加友好，减少对多线程的痛点。对 Python 开发者的异步编程或将带来更易用的新范式。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(426) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nigeltao.github.io/blog/2026/handsum.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Handsum: An LQIP Image File Format</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 05:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48830860' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48830860' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48830860'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Handsum proposes a new image file format optimized for fast low-quality image previews, enabling quicker page paints and smoother progressive loading. The format emphasizes efficient decoding and streaming-friendly buffers to accelerate first paint. If adopted, it could influence how sites balance quality and speed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Handsum 提出了一种用于快速低质量预览的新图像格式，助力提升首屏加载与渐进加载的流畅性。该格式强调高效解码与适合流式缓冲，有望优化网页在质高与速度之间的平衡。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48757619' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(443) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rntz.net/post/2026-06-11-datalog-nontermination.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Evaluation order and nontermination in query languages</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>查询语言中的求值顺序与非终止性</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 02, 2026 / 00:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48757619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48757619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48757619'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post analyzes how evaluation order in query languages like Datalog can lead to nontermination, with implications for database semantics and performance. It discusses termination strategies such as stratification and tabling, and why preserving termination is crucial for predictable queries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章分析了在Datalog等查询语言中求值顺序可能导致的非终止性及其对数据库语义和性能的影响。讨论了如分层、表格化等终止策略，以及为何在可预测查询中保持终止性至关重要。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(446) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://laxmena.com/same-capacity-less-throughput' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Profiling the &quot;Abundance&quot; housing bottleneck with real data</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 05, 2026 / 19:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48799930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 35</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48799930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48799930'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Freno is a cooperative, highly available throttling service used to enforce rate limits across distributed systems. It highlights patterns for reliability and shared ownership in large-scale platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Freno 是一个协作式、高可用的限流服务，用于跨分布式系统执行速率限制，展示了在大规模平台中实现可靠性与协作所有权的模式。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48813246' data-tags='Security,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48813246' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(450) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.alexbeals.com/posts/the-footgun-of-right-to-left-decorative-characters' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The footgun of right-to-left decorative characters</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从右到左装饰字符的坑</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 20:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48813246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 35</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48813246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48813246'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Decorative characters that run RTL can inadvertently affect layout, text rendering, and security-sensitive contexts, creating subtle UX bugs and potential spoofing surfaces. Awareness helps avoid confusing interfaces and security pitfalls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RTL 的装饰字符可能无意中影响布局、文本渲染与安全上下文，带来微妙的用户体验 bug 与潜在的钓鱼表面。文中总结了在标识、界面标签或 URL 中使用此类字符时的具体坑点，提醒开发者避免界面混乱和安全隐患。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.alexbeals.com/images/the-footgun-of-right-to-left-decorative-characters/6.png' data-full='https://blog.alexbeals.com/images/the-footgun-of-right-to-left-decorative-characters/6.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(456) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nanduruganesh.github.io/flash-msa/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flash-MSA: Accelerating Million-Token Training with Sparse Attention Kernels</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flash-MSA：通过稀疏注意力核实现百万级令牌训练加速</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 12, 2026 / 13:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48884618' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 33</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48884618' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48884618'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Presents Flash-MSA, a method to accelerate training of large-token models using sparse attention kernels, reducing memory usage and enabling longer contexts. It discusses performance gains and practical implications for large-scale transformers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 Flash-MSA，利用稀疏注意力核加速百万级令牌模型的训练，显著降低显存并支持更长的上下文。讨论性能提升及对大规模 Transformer 的实际影响。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48825816' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(457) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vatsalbakshi.com/blog/benfords-law/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An interactive explorer for Benford&#x27;s Law across real datasets</a></p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48869243' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48869243' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(460) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://neciudan.dev/most-secure-way-to-store-auth-token' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What&#x27;s the best way to do authentication in modern applications</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>现代应用中的认证最佳实践</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 23:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48869243' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48869243' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48869243'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Modern apps should rely on standards-based authentication flows like OAuth 2.0 / OIDC with PKCE for public clients. Use short-lived access tokens paired with refresh tokens, and consider passwordless options such as WebAuthn to reduce phishing. Store tokens securely (HttpOnly cookies) and enforce MFA and CSRF protections to harden security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>现代应用应依赖基于标准的认证流程，如针对公客户端的 OAuth 2.0 / OIDC + PKCE，并使用短期访问令牌搭配刷新令牌。可以考虑无密码选项如 WebAuthn 以降低钓鱼风险，并将令牌安全存储（HttpOnly cookies），配合 MFA 与 CSRF 防护加强安全性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://neciudan.dev/.netlify/images?url=https%3A%2F%2Fneciudan.dev%2F.netlify%2Fimages%3Furl%3D_astro%252Fauth-token.B0wA1dOJ.png%26fm%3Djpg%26w%3D1200%26h%3D630%26dpl%3D6a5139be902864000875dc83&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;dpl=6a5139be902864000875dc83' data-full='https://neciudan.dev/.netlify/images?url=https%3A%2F%2Fneciudan.dev%2F.netlify%2Fimages%3Furl%3D_astro%252Fauth-token.B0wA1dOJ.png%26fm%3Djpg%26w%3D1200%26h%3D630%26dpl%3D6a5139be902864000875dc83&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;dpl=6a5139be902864000875dc83' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(463) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/sqlsure/sqlsure' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Sqlsure – deterministic semantic checks for AI-generated SQL</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 13:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48875342' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48875342' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48875342'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sqlsure provides deterministic semantic checks for AI-generated SQL, helping ensure queries preserve semantics and safety across deterministic inputs. It supports reproducible testing and auditing of AI-produced SQL.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sqlsure 提供对 AI 生成 SQL 的确定性语义检查，帮助确保查询在语义与安全性方面的一致性。支持对 AI 产出 SQL 进行可重复的测试与审计。</p>

The decision could influence HR practices and dispute resolution processes within the industry. It underscores the importance of clear performance metrics and lawful termination processes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在一起针对某科技公司员工的错误解雇案中，法院认定解雇违法，为科技行业中罕见的员工胜利。此判决可能影响行业的人力资源做法与争议解决流程，强调在解雇时应有明确的绩效指标与合法程序。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(467) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/weirdGuy/kastor' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Kastor – Terraform-style specs for AI agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Kastor — 面向 AI 代理的 Terraform 风格规格</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 08, 2026 / 08:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48833183' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48833183' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48833183'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kastor offers Terraform-style specifications for composing AI agents, enabling declarative definitions of agent capabilities and workflows. This approach could simplify building and coordinating complex AI systems, improving reproducibility and collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kastor 提供了面向 AI 代理的 Terraform 风格规格，使代理能力与工作流的声明性定义成为可能。此做法或将简化复杂 AI 系统的构建与协作，提升可重复性与模块化程度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1d63484f0f8caff7ed94452864b651945244d33562c26bf4893099dd49c8e067/weirdGuy/kastor' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1d63484f0f8caff7ed94452864b651945244d33562c26bf4893099dd49c8e067/weirdGuy/kastor' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(469) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48816813' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: Who wants to be hired&quot; posts outpace &quot;Who&#x27;s hiring&quot; 2 to 1</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tell HN：Who Wants to Be Hired 的帖子数量为何比 Who&#x27;s Hiring 多两倍</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 05:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48816813' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48816813' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48816813'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>On Hacker News, &#x27;Who Wants to Be Hired&#x27; posts now outnumber &#x27;Who&#x27;s Hiring&#x27; by about two to one, signaling demand patterns in the tech job market. The trend may reflect more proactive job-seekers posting themselves rather than employers seeking talent.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 Hacker News 上，‘Who Wants to Be Hired’ 的贴文数量大约是 ‘Who&#x27;s Hiring’ 的两倍，反映了科技招聘市场的需求格局。该趋势可能意味着求职者更积极地自行宣传，而雇主的招聘需求相对下降。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(470) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://debasishg.github.io/blog/part1-cache-conscious-data-layout-in-rust/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cache-Conscious Data Layout in Rust: Field Zoning, False Sharing, 128-Byte Rule</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 中的缓存感知数据布局：字段分区、伪共享与 128 字节规则</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 20:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48813214' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48813214' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48813214'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into cache-conscious data layout in Rust, focusing on field zoning to reduce false sharing and the 128-byte cache-line guideline. The post offers practical layout patterns, illustrating performance gains in concurrent and memory-bound scenarios.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>深入探讨 Rust 的缓存感知数据布局，聚焦字段分区以降低伪共享，并遵循 128 字节缓存行的准则。文中给出实用的布局模式，并展示在并发与内存绑定场景中的性能提升。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48877613' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48877613' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(472) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/1203.5426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Energetic Costs of Cellular Computation (2012)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>细胞计算的能量成本（2012）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 11, 2026 / 18:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48877613' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48877613' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48877613'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An early 2012 study quantifies the energy budget of cellular information processing, framing computing in biological systems in terms of metabolic costs per bit. The work foreshadows energy-aware design for bio-computing and helps benchmarks energy efficiency in neuromorphic ideas.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇 2012 年的研究量化了细胞信息处理的能量预算，将代谢成本与每比特的能耗联系起来。工作为生物计算的能效设计提供基准，并为类脑计算的能耗研究奠定基础。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48818098' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(473) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/bkuan001/halo-record' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Halo – open-source, tamper-evident runtime evidence for AI agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Halo：开源、防篡改的 AI 代理运行时证据</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 07:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48818098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48818098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48818098'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Halo records tamper-evident runtime evidence of AI agent actions, enabling auditing and accountability after the fact. It represents a step toward verifiable provenance in AI systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Halo 能以防篡改的方式记录 AI 代理行动的运行时证据，便于事后审计与问责，是提升 AI 系统可验证溯源性的一个重要步骤。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48814503' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48814503' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(474) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.sturdystatistics.com/posts/api_keys/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why We Don&#x27;t Trust the Database with Authentication</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么我们不信任数据库中的身份验证</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 23:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48814503' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48814503' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48814503'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Databases are ill-suited as the primary authenticators for modern apps, as credentials stored there are high-value and broad-surface targets. The piece argues for externalized identity, short-lived tokens, and defense-in-depth to reduce risk, with recommendations like using OAuth/OIDC and minimizing in-database secrets. It cautions against treating the DB as the trust boundary.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>数据库不应作为应用身份验证的主要执行者，因为存储在数据库中的凭证价值高且暴露面广。文章主张将身份认证外化、使用短寿命令牌，并采用分层防护，如 OAuth/OIDC，减少风险，避免把数据库当作信任边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.sturdystatistics.com/posts/api_keys/petros.jpg' data-full='https://blog.sturdystatistics.com/posts/api_keys/petros.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48819612' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(475) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gerireid.com/blog/organising-documentation-for-humans-and-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Documentation is still in your Mum&#x27;s filing cabinet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>文档仍在妈妈的档案柜里</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 07, 2026 / 08:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48819612' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48819612' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48819612'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Organizing documentation for humans and AI requires clear structure, consistent tone, and searchable indexing. It advocates moving away from hidden, appendix-like docs toward centralized, navigable content with machine-readable metadata so assistants can summarize, answer questions, and generate docs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>为人和 AI 组织文档需要清晰的结构、一致的语气和可检索的索引。它倡导将文档从隐匿的附录转向集中化、可导航的内容，并配以可机器读的元数据，便于助手摘要、回答问题和生成文档。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48881498' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48881498' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(478) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.elliotcsmith.com/autoresearch-claude-and-constrained-optimization/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Autoresearch, Claude and Constrained Optimization</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Autoresearch、Claude 与约束优化</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 12, 2026 / 07:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48881498' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48881498' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48881498'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines how autoresearch workflows using Claude interact with constrained optimization, outlining practical implications for reproducibility, resource budgeting, and control in AI‑assisted work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨在 Claude 支持下的自动化研究如何处理有约束的优化问题，并就可重复性、资源预算与对 AI 助力工作中的控制提出实用见解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.ghost.org/v5.0.0/images/publication-cover.jpg' data-full='https://static.ghost.org/v5.0.0/images/publication-cover.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48805857' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48805857' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(479) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://buildnectar.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Nectar, a Rust-like React that compiles to WebAssembly</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Nectar，一种像 Rust 的 React，能够编译为 WebAssembly</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 06, 2026 / 08:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48805857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48805857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48805857'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nectar is a Rust-like React-like UI library that compiles to WebAssembly, enabling high-performance, low-overhead UI components in the browser with closer-to-the-metal control. It wines a path toward smaller front-ends and tighter integration with systems programming tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nectar 是一个受 Rust 启发、类似 React 的 UI 库，能够编译为 WebAssembly，在浏览器中实现高性能的 UI 组件，开销更小。该方案有望带来更轻量、快速的前端，以及与底层工具链的更紧密整合。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48863849' data-tags='Science,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48863849' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(480) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.atomscale.ai/updates/our-thesis-atom-to-scale' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Materials innovation has a scale-up problem, not discovery</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>材料创新的问题在于放大阶段，而非发现阶段</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jul 10, 2026 / 12:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48863849' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48863849' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48863849'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The claim is that the bottleneck in materials innovation lies in scaling from lab to production, not discovery. The post argues for investments in scalable synthesis, manufacturing, and supply chains to realize breakthroughs, rather than solely modeling or high-throughput discovery. It emphasizes operational challenges that slow technology transfer.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为材料创新的瓶颈在于从实验室到生产的放大阶段，而非新材料的发现本身。文章呼吁加强可扩展的合成、制造与供应链等能力，来真正实现突破，而不仅仅依赖理论建模或高通量发现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://atomscale.ai/static/logos/AtomscaleOGImage.png' data-full='https://atomscale.ai/static/logos/AtomscaleOGImage.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
